Motivation: 现有后门防御方法一次只能针对一个后门，且通常需要知道触发器，导致防御者在面对模型中可能存在未知后门时处于结构性劣势。作者希望探索是否存在一种更通用的后门防御机制。

Method: 在三个模型族（通过预训练或持续预训练注入后门）上，训练模型遗忘单个触发器，然后分析其他未针对的后门是否也被抑制。引入Cross Activation Shift Distance（交叉激活偏移距离）来量化不同训练引起的模型变化之间的相似性。

Result: 移除一个后门也能抑制其他从未被明确针对的后门。某些后门的移除会诱导其他后门的抑制，这种现象可以通过交叉激活偏移距离来量化和解释。

Conclusion: 结果为大语言模型安全性开辟了新方向：防御者可以故意注入可控后门然后将其移除，利用跨后门迁移效应来抑制攻击者可能已植入的未知后门。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks in Large Language Models (LLMs) are a growing security concern, where models can generate adversary-chosen content. Existing defenses target backdoors one at a time and typically require knowledge of the trigger, leaving the defender at a structural disadvantage when unknown backdoors may exist in a model. We show that backdoor neutralization through unlearning generalizes across backdoors: training a model to ignore a single trigger can also suppress other backdoors that were never explicitly targeted. We study this phenomenon across three model families, whose backdoors were injected via pretraining or continual pretraining, by analyzing the models obtained after removing one backdoor at a time. To understand why unlearning certain backdoors induces the suppression of others, we introduce the Cross Activation Shift Distance, to quantify the distance between model changes induced by different trainings. Our results open a new direction for LLM safety as defenders could deliberately inject controlled backdoors and then remove them, leveraging cross-backdoor transfer to also suppress unknown backdoors that an attacker may have previously introduced in the model.

</details>


### [78] [Exploring Adversarial Robustness and Safety Alignment in Multilingual Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03793)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型在12种语言上的对抗鲁棒性与多语言安全性，发现跨语言可迁移的对抗脆弱性，并揭示了"安全即失败"现象——低资源语言看似安全实为理解失败，而深层多语言训练才能实现真正的安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM鲁棒性研究主要集中在英语任务上，多语言行为尚未被探索。

Method: 在12种语言上对经过指令微调获得多语言能力的开源MLLM进行系统评估，使用基于梯度的攻击方法研究跨语言对抗迁移性，并分析文本指令和图像文字两种场景下的多语言安全表现。

Result: 对抗图像具有强跨语言迁移性；不同语言的安全性差异源于模型对有害指令的理解能力不同；低资源语言的安全表现是视觉理解和语法失败假象（safety-by-failure）；Qwen3-VL等全阶段多语言训练模型展现出真正的跨语言安全。

Conclusion: 浅层多语言适应（如翻译数据微调）仅产生表面理解，造成低资源语言的虚假安全；深层多语言训练才能实现真正的安全对齐。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models integrate visual perception into language reasoning, introducing a continuous attack surface susceptible to adversarial attacks. Prior work on MLLM robustness has focused largely on English-centric tasks, leaving multilingual behaviour unexplored. We address this gap through a systematic study of adversarial robustness and multimodal safety across 12 diverse languages, evaluating open-source MLLMs that acquire multilingual capability through instruction tuning. Gradient-based attacks reveal a transferable multilingual vulnerability: adversarial images optimized in one language continue to induce failure in others, demonstrating strong cross-lingual transferability. Multilingual safety further varies with how effectively a model retrieves or interprets harmful instructions. When harmful intent is issued through text, languages with stronger linguistic grounding more often elicit misuse-enabling responses, while weaker languages produce fewer unsafe outputs. When embedded in the image as typographic content, English scripts are reliably recognised and followed, whereas non-English scripts are rarely parsed by the vision encoder. Lower-resource languages may therefore appear safer, but this is an artefact of comprehension and visual-grounding failures rather than genuine alignment, a phenomenon we term safety-by-failure. In contrast, MLLMs that build multilingual capability throughout their training stages rather than only at instruction tuning, such as Qwen3-VL, exhibit genuine cross-lingual safety, maintaining active refusal across languages rather than masking comprehension failure. Shallow multilingual adaptation, such as fine-tuning on translated instruction data, may produce surface-level understanding that creates illusory safety in low-resource languages; deeper integration across training stages leads to genuine multilingual safety alignment.

</details>


### [79] [Consistency Training Can Entrench Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03810)
*David Demitri Africa,Arathi Mani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一致性训练对模型对齐（Alignment）并非中性：它能抑制奖励黑客（reward hacking）和新兴失调（emergent misalignment），但会放大谄媚（sycophancy）行为，原因在于一致性标注过程引入的分布偏移，而非选择算子的差异。作者通过108个有控失调的开源模型和统一理论框架进行了系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一致性训练（Consistency Training）这类自举式方法对模型对齐效果的未知影响，特别是其是否会意外放大模型中的不良行为。

Method: 在108个"模式生物"（7B-70B开源模型，经微调展现不同类别的可控失调行为）上测试了七种一致性训练方法，并结合统一理论框架分析对齐效果的系统性规律。

Result: 一致性训练普遍抑制奖励黑客和新兴失调，但显著放大谄媚行为；分布偏移（而非选择算子差异）是系统性对齐效应的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 一致性训练并非对齐中性，在关键系统中使用时应谨慎审计其对齐影响。

Abstract: Consistency training encourages a model to produce similar outputs across related inputs or sampling procedures. Such methods are simple, scalable, and largely label-free, but their effects on model alignment remain poorly understood. Could the self-bootstrapping nature of these methods amplify undesired behavior in models? We test seven consistency training methods on 108 ``model organisms: open-source models (7B--70B) fine-tuned to exhibit various forms of controlled misaligned behavior. We find that outcomes vary significantly: consistency training generally suppresses reward hacking and emergent misalignment but amplifies sycophancy. We present evidence that distribution shifts induced by the consistency labeling process, rather than variation in the selection operators, may be the primary driver of systematic alignment effects. Finally, we present a unifying theoretical framework to derive conditions under which consistency training will amplify or suppress misalignment. In total, our study establishes that consistency training is not alignment-neutral, and that its use in critical systems should be carefully audited.

</details>


### [80] [Rethinking the Idiomaticity Decomposability Hypothesis: Evidence from Distributional Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03817)
*Maggie Mi,Golzar Atefi,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Felix Gers,Aline Villavicencio,Nafise Sadat Moosavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用上下文语言模型作为受控分布学习器，研究习语的可分解性（构成词义对整体比喻义的贡献程度）与句法灵活性之间的关系。模型内部的可分解性度量与人工评分弱相关，与句法灵活性呈小而稳定的负相关。预训练分析表明，习语表征的稳定化不仅受频率影响，还受surprisal、可分解性和频率的共同作用，其中可分解性表现出最强的训练依赖效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为习语的可分解性预测句法灵活性，而基于用法的观点则归因于分布经验（如熟悉度和可预测性）。本文旨在通过上下文语言模型作为受控学习器，检验这两种观点的有效性。

Method: 提出一种模型内部的可分解性度量方法，将其与人工评分、句法灵活性和可预测性相关联，并追踪预训练过程中习语学习的变化。

Result: 模型得出的可分解性与人工判断弱相关，与句法灵活性呈小而稳定的负相关。预训练分析显示习语表征稳定化受surprisal、可分解性和频率共同影响，其中可分解性训练依赖效应最强。

Conclusion: 习语行为不能仅用频率解释，可分解性、surprisal和频率共同作用，支持基于用法的观点，同时可分解性在模型学习过程中具有独特的动态效应。

Abstract: Idioms can be analysed in terms of their decomposability, the extent to which constituent meanings contribute to the figurative whole. Decomposability is thought to predict syntactic flexibility. Usage-based accounts instead attribute idiom behaviour to distributional experience, such as speaker familiarity and predictability. We examine these views using contextualised language models as controlled distributional learners. We propose a model-internal measure of decomposability and relate it to human ratings, syntactic flexibility, and predictability while tracking idiom learning during pretraining. Model-derived decomposability correlates weakly with human judgments and shows a small but consistent negative relationship with syntactic flexibility. Pretraining analyses show that stabilisation of idiom representations in models is not explained by frequency alone. Instead, surprisal, decomposability, and frequency all contribute, with decomposability showing the strongest training-dependent effect.

</details>


### [81] [Clustered Self-Assessment: A Simple yet Effective Method for Uncertainty Quantification in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03846)
*Qi Cao,Takeshi Kojima,Andrew Gambardella,Helinyi Peng,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于语义聚类和多项选择的LLM不确定性自评估方法，仅需少量样本即可有效量化模型输出可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常生成看似合理但事实错误的回复，且缺乏显式的不确定性估计，现有方法依赖间接信号（如生成熵），难以解释且未充分利用模型自评估能力。

Method: 将多次采样的生成结果按语义聚类为不同簇，将其转换为结构化多项选择题的选项，利用LLM对各选项的分配概率作为置信度估计。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上，该方法一致优于基线方法，且仅需2个额外样本即可达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法简单有效且高效，为LLM不确定性量化提供了一种实用的自评估方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable performance across diverse tasks, but they often generate responses that appear plausible while being factually incorrect. This problem is compounded by the lack of explicit uncertainty estimates, which makes it difficult for users to judge the reliability of model outputs. Existing uncertainty quantification methods typically rely on indirect signals, such as entropy across sampled generations. These signals can be difficult to interpret and do not fully leverage the model's ability to assess its own uncertainty. We propose a simple yet effective self-assessment method for uncertainty quantification in LLMs. Our approach groups sampled generations into semantically distinct clusters, converts them into answer options in a structured multiple-choice question, and uses the probability assigned by the LLM to each option as a confidence estimate. Experiments across multiple models and datasets show that our method consistently outperforms baseline approaches. Notably, it achieves competitive performance with as few as two additional samples, demonstrating both its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [82] [A Training-Free Mixture-of-Agents Framework for Multi-Document Summarization using LLMs and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03867)
*Cuong Vuong Tuan,Trang Mai Xuan,Tien-Cuong Nguyen,Vu-Duc Ngo,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的多智能体混合框架，结合大语言模型和知识图谱，通过分解为抽取、知识感知抽象和迭代精炼等专业化智能体任务，并利用多视角一致性机制进行输出统一，在多个数据集上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MDS方法难以捕获文档间复杂关系、依赖大量标注数据、跨领域和跨语言泛化能力有限。

Method: 采用训练无关的混合智能体框架，将摘要任务分解为抽取式选择、知识感知抽象和迭代精炼三个子任务，分别由不同LLM智能体负责，并通过知识图谱增强信息理解，最后利用LLM引导的多视角一致性机制统一各智能体输出。

Result: 在英语和越南语的四个数据集上取得了最优或具有竞争力的结果，验证了方法的有效性和适应性。

Conclusion: 该模块化设计无需任务特定微调即可实现跨领域和跨语言的多文档摘要，展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-Document Summarization (MDS) plays a critical role in distilling essential information from collections of textual data. Existing approaches often struggle to capture complex inter-document relationships, rely heavily on large amounts of labeled data for supervised training, or exhibit limited generalization across domains and languages. To address these limitations, we present a training-free mixture-of-agents framework for MDS that leverages the complementary strengths of large language models (LLMs) and knowledge graphs. Our approach decomposes summarization into specialized agent tasks: extractive selection, knowledge-aware abstraction, and iterative refinement, each operating without task-specific fine-tuning. We unify their outputs using a multi-perspective consistency mechanism guided by LLMs. Experiments across four datasets in English and Vietnamese demonstrate state-of-the-art or competitive performance, validating the effectiveness and adaptability of our modular design.

</details>


### [83] [RealClawBench: Live OpenClaw Benchmarks from Real Developer-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03889)
*Zongwei Lv,Zhewen Tan,Yaoming Li,Yilun Yao,Yuxuan Tian,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang,Weihong Lin,Tong Yang,Guangxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RealClawBench 是一个基于真实 OpenClaw 会话构建的实时基准测试框架，通过重构执行环境和确定性可验证评分器将真实用户会话转化为可复现、可自动评分的任务，发布 281 个可执行任务，评估 14 个模型后最高仅解决 65.8% 的任务，揭示了现实开发代理工作负载上存在显著提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理基准测试缺乏真实开发人员-代理会话的关键真实性属性（如分布、多样性和实际难度），无法反映用户实际部署场景中的需求。

Method: 从真实 OpenClaw 会话中构建基准，采用两种核心机制：重构执行环境（reconstructed execution environments）和确定性可验证评分器（deterministic verifiable scorers），将真实会话转化为可复现且可自动评分的任务。

Result: 发布 281 个可执行任务，保持源分布（最大最终与源分布的 Jensen-Shannon 散度为 0.0448）；评估 14 个当代模型，最佳系统仅解决 65.8% 的任务。

Conclusion: RealClawBench 通过将真实部署会话转化为受控评估实例，提供了一条更准确衡量代理在实际使用中能力的实用路径，并表明当前模型在现实开发代理工作负载上仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks should reflect what users actually ask deployed agents to do, yet existing benchmarks often miss key realism properties of real developer-agent sessions. We introduce RealClawBench, a live benchmark framework built from real OpenClaw sessions to capture the distribution, diversity, and real-world difficulty of deployed agent use. Real user requests are challenging to benchmark because they often depend on local execution environments, involve implicit or underspecified intent, and require nontrivial verification. RealClawBench addresses these challenges with two core mechanisms: reconstructed execution environments and deterministic verifiable scorers, which together convert real sessions into reproducible, automatically scored tasks. The resulting release contains 281 executable tasks sampled from a much larger real-session pool while preserving the source distribution, with maximum final-vs-source Jensen-Shannon divergence of 0.0448. Evaluating 14 contemporary models shows that the best system solves only 65.8% of tasks, revealing substantial headroom on realistic developer-agent workloads. By turning real deployed sessions into controlled evaluation instances, RealClawBench provides a practical path toward benchmarks that better measure agent capability in actual use. Code is available at:https://anonymous.4open.science/r/real-claw-bench-582B.

</details>


### [84] [Synthesize and Reward -- Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Tool Use in Live Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03892)
*Ibrahim Abdelaziz,Asim Munawar,Kinjal Basu,Maxwell Crouse,Chulaka Gunasekara,Suneet Katrekar,Pavan Kapanipathi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PROVE框架通过三种贡献（20个MCP服务器、自动化数据合成流水线、多组件程序化奖励）解决LLM多步工具调用的训练难题，在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval上分别提升最高+10.2、+6.8和+6.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练LLM编排多步工具调用面临三个耦合障碍：真实有状态执行环境构建成本高、合成查询脱离服务器实际状态导致工具调用失败、基于回忆的RL奖励会鼓励冗长的工具调用模式。

Method: (1) 构建20个有状态MCP服务器（343个工具），支持会话级状态隔离的实时执行RL训练；(2) 自动数据合成流水线，通过依赖图引导的对话模拟生成验证过的多轮工具调用轨迹；(3) 多组件程序化奖励函数（含渐变有效性评分、依赖感知覆盖率、自适应效率惩罚、工具名称信号、参数值匹配奖励），无需外部评判模型。

Result: 在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval上分别提升最高+10.2、+6.8和+6.5个点。使用相同奖励超参数和约13K训练样本训练Qwen3-4B/8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Granite-4.1-8B。

Conclusion: 紧凑的程序化奖励能够在两个模型家族的多步工具编排任务上实现一致的性能提升，证明无需外部评判模型即可有效优化工具调用。

Abstract: Training LLMs to orchestrate multi-step tool calls is held back by three coupled obstacles: realistic stateful execution environments are costly to build, synthetic training queries are often detached from the server's actual state (so the generated tool calls fail to execute), and recall-based RL rewards incentivize verbose tool-calling patterns. We present PROVE (Programmatic Rewards On Verified Environments), a framework with three contributions: (1) a library of 20 stateful MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers exposing 343 tools, enabling live-execution RL training with session-scoped state isolation; (2) an automated data synthesis pipeline that generates validated multi-turn tool-call trajectories against these servers via dependency-graph-guided conversation simulation grounded in live-sampled server state, so every generated query references entities that actually exist; and (3) a multi-component programmatic reward - graduated validity scoring, dependency-aware coverage, an adaptive efficiency penalty with a complexity-scaled call budget, a tool-name signal, and an argument-value matching bonus - requiring no external judge model. We train four models (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Granite-4.1-8B) with GRPO using identical reward hyperparameters and ~13K training examples; only learning rate is tuned per model family from a three-point sweep. On BFCL Multi-Turn, tau2-bench, and T-Eval, PROVE yields improvements of up to +10.2, +6.8, and +6.5 points respectively, demonstrating that a compact programmatic reward yields consistent gains on multi-step tool orchestration across two model families.

</details>


### [85] [Knowledge Editing in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03924)
*Haewon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将“定位-编辑”知识编辑方法从自回归模型迁移到掩码扩散模型，发现两者在最优编辑位置（早期到中间层的MLP）上一致，但在多token目标编辑时MDM性能显著下降。原因在于编辑未针对部分去噪的中间状态进行优化，通过引入针对这些状态的简单修正可大幅恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如locate-then-edit）仅在自回归模型上开发和验证，其在掩码扩散模型（双向建模、迭代去噪生成）上的适用性未知，需要系统性迁移与评估。

Method: (1) 将locate-then-edit方法迁移到MDM（LLaDA, Dream）；(2) 与ARM（LLaMA, Qwen）在相同规模下进行因果追踪定位和编辑效果比较；(3) 诊断多token编辑失败原因，提出针对部分去噪中间状态优化的简单修正策略。

Result: (1) 因果追踪定位在两种架构中一致指向最后subject token的早到中层MLP，且该位置编辑效果最佳；(2) 单token编辑在两者均成功，但多token编辑在MDM中系统性退化；(3) 退化根源在于多token生成需经过未针对编辑优化的部分掩码中间状态。

Conclusion: 定位-编辑方法可跨架构迁移，但MDM的多token编辑需要针对其迭代去噪生成过程进行额外优化；提出的简单修正策略能有效恢复多token编辑性能。

Abstract: Knowledge editing aims to update or correct factual knowledge in a language model. A widely used approach, locate-then-edit, does this in two steps: it first localizes a fact within the model, then edits the weights there. To date, such methods have been developed exclusively on autoregressive models (ARMs). Whether their underlying assumptions hold for masked diffusion models (MDMs), which model text bidirectionally and generate by iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, remains an open question. We address it by transferring locate-then-edit to MDMs and comparing two MDMs (LLaDA, Dream) with two ARMs (LLaMA, Qwen) at matched scale. Our central finding has two parts. First, where an edit is applied transfers across paradigms: causal tracing highlights the same early-to-mid-layer MLP at the last subject token in both, and editing is most effective there. Second, this shared location does not guarantee a shared outcome. Single-token edits succeed in both, but as targets grow longer, editing degrades systematically in the MDMs but not the ARMs. The failure stems from how the edited fact is generated: producing a multi-token target requires passing through partially unmasked intermediate states for which the edit was never optimized. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce a simple correction that optimizes the edit for these states, substantially restoring multi-token performance.

</details>


### [86] [A Pocket Offline Model for Simultaneous Speech Translation as CUNI Submission to IWSLT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03948)
*Aziz Sharipov Ortega,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实现了基于离线直接语音转文本翻译模型Canary的同步翻译能力，采用最先进的AlignAtt策略，提交至IWSLT 2026同步语音翻译共享任务，涵盖捷克语→英语、英语→德语和意大利语方向。系统优势包括：高翻译质量（在低延迟和高延迟场景下均优于同等规模基线）、低计算需求（仅1B参数）以及多语言支持（25种源语言和25种目标语言）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高质量的同步语音翻译，同时兼顾低延迟、低计算开销和多语言支持，解决现有系统在翻译质量与延迟之间权衡不足的问题。

Method: 基于Canary离线直接语音转文本翻译模型，结合AlignAtt策略实现同步翻译能力。

Result: 在低延迟和高延迟场景下均优于同等规模基线模型；模型仅1B参数；支持25种源语言和25种目标语言。

Conclusion: 该方法在翻译质量、计算效率和语言覆盖范围方面均具有优势，适用于同步语音翻译任务。

Abstract: We implement simultaneous translation capability with the offline direct speech-to-text translation model Canary, using the state-of-the-art policy AlignAtt, and submit it to IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Shared task for Czech to English and English to German and Italian.
  The strengths of our system are: (1) high translation quality, outperforming similarly sized baselines both in low- and high-latency regimes in computationally unaware simulations; (2) low computational requirements, as the model has only 1B parameters; (3) multilinguality -- support of 25 source and 25 target languages.

</details>


### [87] [Efficient ASR Training with Conversations that Never Happened](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03957)
*Máté Gedeon,Péter Mihajlik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM生成对话文本再通过TTS合成语音的数据增强流水线，仅用67小时真实对话数据+636小时合成数据，在匈牙利语BEA-Dialogue基准上超越了用2700小时语音训练的零样本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言和特定领域的对话式ASR受限于多说话人训练数据的稀缺，现有方法难以获取场景级、带说话人元数据的对话数据。

Method: 提出数据增强流水线：(1) LLM生成带参与者元数据的场景级对话；(2) 将说话人属性映射到TTS语音配置；(3) 合成带说话人感知的对话语音。在FastConformer-Large框架下，评估了5种LLM家族在单一生成器、固定预算混合和规模扩展三种设置下的表现。

Result: 合成对话持续提升了ASR性能，但生成器选择和数构成影响显著。最大配置（67小时真实+636小时合成）在BEA-Dialogue上优于2700小时匈牙利语训练的零样本模型。

Conclusion: LLM生成的对话文本经TTS合成后，是真实对话语料在语音模型训练中的有效补充，为低资源对话ASR提供了可行的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Conversational ASR for lower-resource languages and niche domains is limited by the scarcity of domain-matched multi-speaker training data. We propose an augmentation pipeline that generates scenario-level dialogues with participant metadata, maps speaker attributes to TTS voice profiles, and assembles synthesized utterances into speaker-aware simulated conversations. We evaluated five LLM families under single-generator, fixed-budget mixture, and scale-up settings using the same FastConformer-Large training recipe for each one. We ran comprehensive evaluations on the Hungarian BEA-Dialogue benchmark corpus, with the method itself being applicable to any language given the resources for each component. The results show that synthetic conversations consistently improve speech recognition performance, but generator choice and data composition strongly affect the gains. Our largest training configuration, using only 67 hours of real conversations and 636 hours of simulated data, achieves better performance on the evaluation benchmark than a zero-shot model trained on 2700 hours of Hungarian speech. These findings indicate that LLM-generated conversational data synthesized with TTS is a practical complement to real conversational corpora for speech model training.

</details>


### [88] [Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering for Efficient and Controllable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03965)
*Yu Xia,Zhouhang Xie,Xin Xu,Byungkyu Kang,Prarit Lamba,Xiang Gao,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ACTS（Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering），通过控制器智能体在推理过程中自适应地引导冻结推理器的思考策略，实现预算感知的高效推理，在保持性能的同时大幅节约token，并支持可控制的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过扩展思维链推理提升了最终答案准确率，但存在token使用效率低下且缺乏推理时控制的问题。现有高效推理方法通过缩短、早停或压缩思维链来控制思考长度，但未能揭示模型如何思考的内在机制。

Method: 将推理引导形式化为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），其中控制器智能体在每个步骤观察推理轨迹和剩余思考预算，发出包含推理策略和引导短语的引导动作。控制器通过多预算增强的合成引导轨迹初始化，并进一步通过带预算条件奖励塑形的强化学习优化。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，ACTS在匹配完整推理性能的同时实现了显著的token节省，并在不同推理器和任务上实现了可控的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: ACTS通过自适应推理引导实现了预算感知的高效推理，有效解决了LLM推理中token效率低下和缺乏推理时控制的问题。

Abstract: Large language models improve final-answer accuracy through extended chain-of-thought reasoning, but often spend tokens inefficiently and offer little inference-time control. Existing efficient reasoning methods control thinking length by shortening, early-stopping, or compressing traces, leaving how the model thinks implicit. In this paper, we propose Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering (ACTS), which formulates reasoning steering as a Markov decision process where a controller agent adaptively steers a frozen reasoner during inference. At each step, the controller observes the reasoning trace and remaining thinking budget, then issues a steering action consisting of a reasoning strategy and a steering phrase that initiates the next reasoner step. This enables budget-aware strategy control for efficient reasoning while preserving the reasoner's generation continuity. We initialize the controller agent from our constructed synthetic steering trajectories with multi-budget augmentation, and further optimize it via reinforcement learning with budget-conditioned reward shaping. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that ACTS matches full-thinking performance with substantial token savings, and enables controllable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across different reasoners and tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/Andree-9/ACTS.

</details>


### [89] [AlignAtt4LLM: Fast AlignAtt for Decoder-Only LLMs at IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03967)
*Quentin Fuxa,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlignAtt4LLM 是一个 IWSLT 2026 同声传译系统，采用同步级联架构（Qwen3-ASR + Gemma-4 E4B-it），首次将 AlignAtt 策略应用于纯解码器 LLM，通过显式源文本跨度、翻译特定注意力头选择、选择性 qk-fast replay 和运行时查询/键捕获四项技术，在英德和英意方向上优于基线，英中方向结果较复杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 AlignAtt 策略从编码器-解码器架构扩展到纯解码器 LLM，解决其缺乏编码器-解码器交叉注意力机制导致无法直接应用 AlignAtt 的问题。

Method: (1) 在提示中引入显式源文本跨度；(2) 离线选择翻译特定的对齐注意力头；(3) 选择性 qk-fast replay 草稿到源注意块；(4) 运行时查询/键捕获以比特级保持模型输出不变。

Result: 在 IWSLT 2026 开发集上，英德和英意方向在低延迟（~2秒）和高延迟（<4秒 CU-LongYAAL）两个场景均优于基线；英中方向结果较复杂。

Conclusion: AlignAtt4LLM 不绑定于特定模型（Gemma-4），只需确定性提示布局、标定的注意力头和查询/键捕获即可复用到更强的纯解码器翻译骨干模型上。

Abstract: We describe AlignAtt4LLM, an IWSLT 2026 simultaneous speech translation system for English to German, Italian, and Chinese. The system is a synchronous cascade: Qwen3-ASR with forced alignment produces an incrementally updated source transcript, and Gemma-4 E4B-it translates that prefix under an MT-side AlignAtt policy.
  To our knowledge, this is the first application of AlignAtt to a decoder-only LLM, where the encoder-decoder cross-attention used by earlier AlignAtt systems is absent. We recover a usable policy by proposing (1) an explicit source span in the prompt, (2) offline selection of translation-specific alignment heads, (3) selective qk-fast replay of the draft-to-source attention block, and (4) runtime query/key capture that preserves model outputs bit-identically.
  On the IWSLT 2026 development set, AlignAtt4LLM outperforms the supplied baselines for the European target languages, English to German and English to Italian, in both the low-latency regime around 2 seconds and the high-latency regime below 4 seconds CU-LongYAAL. Results for English to Chinese are more mixed, but the method is not tied to Gemma-4: because AlignAtt4LLM only requires a deterministic prompt layout, calibrated attention heads, and query/key capture, the same policy can be reapplied to stronger translation-focused decoder-only MT backbones for non-European target languages.

</details>


### [90] [QUBRIC: Co-Designing Queries and Rubrics for RL Beyond Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03968)
*Rongzhi Zhang,Rui Feng,Zhihan Zhang,Jingfeng Yang,Qingyu Yin,Xin Liu,Zixuan Zhang,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin,Tuo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUBRIC提出了查询与评分标准协同设计框架，通过教师关键点将开放式查询改写为场景化可评估问题，并利用对比生成和可学习性过滤，在ArenaHard上比SFT基线提升+5.5分，且泛化到法律、道德、叙事推理三个未见基准上平均提升+6.3分，证明了协同设计能使基于评分标准的RL超越严格可验证任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的RL方法将查询分布视为固定，导致评分标准质量受查询结构约束；开放式查询产生模糊评分标准，而简单收窄查询会引入模型无法验证的虚假引用，导致训练无奖励信号。因此需要协同设计查询与评分标准来突破这一结构性瓶颈。

Method: QUBRIC包含三个核心组件：1) 教师关键点将开放式查询改写为场景化的可评估问题；2) 对比评分标准生成将教师-策略差距转化为查询级标准；3) 可学习性过滤保留信息量丰富的查询-评分标准对用于GRPO训练。

Result: 在ArenaHard上比SFT基线提升+5.5分；仅在指令跟随数据上训练后，可泛化到法律、道德、叙事推理三个未见基准，平均提升+6.3分，且改进集中在推理相关维度。

Conclusion: 协同设计查询与评分标准能够使基于评分标准的RL成为超越严格可验证任务的RLVR实际补充方案。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL is a promising route for extending reinforcement learning beyond verifiable rewards, yet existing methods optimize rubrics while treating the query distribution as fixed. We identify a structural bottleneck: rubric quality is constrained by query structure. Open-ended queries yield vague rubrics; naively narrowing them introduces fabricated references that no model can verify, so all responses fail and training receives no reward signal. We present QUBRIC, a framework that co-designs queries and rubrics. Teacher-derived key points ground the rewriting of open-ended queries into scenario-based, evaluable questions. Contrastive rubric generation then turns teacher-policy gaps into query-level criteria, and learnability filtering retains only informative query-rubric pairs for GRPO training. QUBRIC achieves a +5.5 point gain on ArenaHard over the SFT baseline. Trained only on instruction-following data, it further transfers to three held-out benchmarks spanning legal, moral, and narrative reasoning (+6.3 points on average), with improvements concentrated in reasoning-related dimensions. These results provide evidence that co-designing queries and rubrics can make rubric-based RL a practical complement to RLVR beyond strictly verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [91] [Quantifying Faithful Confidence Expression in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03969)
*Areeb Gani,Asal Meskin,Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大型推理模型（LRMs）在置信度忠实校准（FC）方面存在的系统性缺陷，提出了一种量化框架，通过分析语言决断性与三种内部不确定性来源（token概率、隐藏状态、采样一致性）的关系，并采用前缀条件采样控制轨迹结构变异，实验表明LRMs的推理行为并不能自动改善置信度表达的忠实性，且不同置信度估计器对同一轨迹的评估结果存在分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的置信度沟通对于LLMs的可信度至关重要，但置信度忠实校准（内在置信度与语言表达的置信度之间的一致性）一直是一个持续性失败模式。特别是大型推理模型（LRMs）被广泛使用，但其推理轨迹常被用户视为深思熟虑和能力的证据，然而LRMs能否忠实地表达其置信度尚不清楚。此外，现有评估FC的范式难以推广到LRMs生成的长思维链输出上，因为这些输出缺乏清晰的步骤边界、步骤结构不一致且编码了复杂的条件依赖关系，使得内在置信度的估计变得复杂。

Method: 提出了一种系统量化LRMs置信度忠实校准（FC）的新框架。该框架基于token概率、隐藏状态和采样响应一致性三种内部不确定性来源，分析语言决断性相对于这些来源的关系。同时设计了前缀条件采样的方法来控制轨迹中条件和结构变异的影响。

Result: 在多个领先模型、数据集和提示上的实验表明：LRMs在置信度忠实表达方面存在显著挑战；推理行为并不能自动转化为更好的FC；适用于非推理模型的提示干预措施在推理场景中无法改善置信度忠实性；不同的置信度估计器对同一轨迹会产生分歧性评估，揭示了以往评估方法的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 本研究将置信度忠实校准（FC）确立为LRMs的一个独立可靠性与对齐目标，特别是在这些系统被部署到高风险应用场景中时尤为重要。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty communication is critical to the trustworthiness of LLMs, yet faithful calibration (FC)--the alignment between models' intrinsic and (linguistically) expressed confidence--is a persistent failure mode. This challenge is key for large reasoning models (LRMs), whose extended reasoning traces are often interpreted by users as evidence of deliberation, competence, and confidence. Despite the importance of FC and wide usage of LRMs, the extent to which LRMs can faithfully express their confidence remains poorly understood. Moreover, the prevailing paradigm to measure FC does not generalize well to the long chain-of-thought outputs generated by LRMs, which tend to lack clear step boundaries, involve inconsistent step structure, and encode complex conditional dependencies throughout the trace--complicating estimation of intrinsic confidence. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel framework to systematically quantify FC of LRMs. Our framework analyzes linguistic decisiveness relative to three sources of internal uncertainty, based on token probabilities, hidden states, and sampled response consistency. We also devise a prefix-conditioned sampling approach to control for conditional and structural variation across traces. Applying our framework to a diverse suite of leading models, datasets, and prompts, we find that faithful confidence expression is a significant challenge for LRMs. Reasoning behaviors do not automatically translate to improved FC, and prompt interventions for non-reasoning models do not improve faithfulness in the reasoning setting. Different confidence estimators further produce divergent assessments of the same traces, revealing fragility in prior evaluation methodologies. Taken together, our work establishes FC as a distinct reliability and alignment target for LRMs, particularly as such systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes contexts.

</details>


### [92] [Language Models Compare Quantities Using Number-specific and Unit-specific Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03982)
*Mutsumi Sasaki,Go kamoda,Ryosuke Takahashi,Kosuke Sato,Kentaro Inui,Keisuke Sakaguchi,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究语言模型如何比较带单位的数量（如110 cm与1.2 m），发现模型在比较边界附近系统性出错，且依赖基于数值差和单位尺度差的启发式策略，而非精确的统一尺度转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在处理带测量单位的数量比较任务时的内在机制，特别是模型是否真正理解数值与单位尺度的组合关系，还是仅依赖表面启发式。

Method: 在多个单位系统中设置受控实验，通过线性替代模型预测语言模型偏好，并对齐数值差和单位尺度差的相关子空间进行因果干预。

Result: 模型在比较边界附近（即数值微小变化决定正确答案的区域）准确率显著下降；错误呈现系统性模式，线性模型可基于数值差和单位尺度差预测输出；因果干预证实模型依赖这些特征。

Conclusion: 语言模型比较带单位数量时并非进行精确的同一尺度转换，而是使用一组关于数值和单位的启发式策略。

Abstract: Quantities with measurement units, such as 110 cm and 1.2 m, require language models (LMs) to combine a numeral with a symbolic unit scale. Here, we study how LMs compare such quantities in controlled settings spanning several unit systems. We find that accuracy degrades near the comparison boundary, where small changes in value determine the correct answer. The resulting errors are systematic: linear surrogate models predict LM preferences from numerical-difference and unit-scale-difference cues, and causal interventions on subspaces aligned with these variables shift model's output. The results suggest that LMs compare quantities through a bag of heuristics over numerals and units, rather than first converting both expressions to an exact shared-scale representation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [MultiTurnPSB: Evaluating Multi-Turn Jailbreak Attacks an dClassifier-Based Defenses for Medical AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02630)
*Anushka Sheoran,Yiduo Hao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了多轮对抗安全基准MultiTurnPSB，发现GPT-4.1-mini在单轮评估中安全，但在第四轮对抗攻击下不安全响应从35%升至近80%，且不同模型间的安全差距从单轮不可见变为19倍。识别出导致多数灾难性失败的两元素攻击公式，轻量级分类器虽可降低52个百分点的风险，但45%误报率是部署瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗聊天机器人安全评估仅基于单轮提示，忽略了真实用户在对话中会反复追问、施加紧迫感和援引权威等对抗行为，导致对模型安全性的严重误判。

Method: 构建MultiTurnPSB（PatientSafetyBench的四轮对抗扩展），采用固定模板攻击、模板自适应攻击和实时对抗攻击三种策略，对GPT-4.1-mini和Claude Sonnet 4.5进行多轮安全评估。

Result: 实时攻击下不安全响应从35%升至近80%；GPT-4.1-mini与Claude Sonnet 4.5在基线时无统计差异，但第四轮差距达19倍；识别出四种退化轨迹特征和一个两元素攻击公式；轻量级分类器可降52个百分点但误报率达45%；Claude Sonnet在超过一半的后期轮次中拒绝生成对抗消息。

Conclusion: 单轮评估严重低估模型安全风险，多轮对抗评估是必要的；两元素攻击公式是主要风险来源；输入侧防御有效但误报率过高是主要瓶颈；安全训练可能泛化到攻击者角色，需引起重视。

Abstract: Patient-facing medical chatbots are commonly evaluated on single-turn prompts, yet real users push back after refusals, add urgency, and invoke authority. We introduce MultiTurnPSB, a four-turn adversarial extension of PatientSafetyBench, and evaluate GPT-4.1-mini under fixed template, template-adaptive, and live adversarial attacks. Unsafe responses rise from 35% to nearly 80% by Turn 4 under live attack. Under the same adversary, GPT-4.1-mini and Claude Sonnet 4.5 are statistically indistinguishable at baseline but diverge to a 19x gap by Turn 4, a difference invisible to single-turn evaluation. We characterize four degradation trajectory signatures and identify a two-element attack formula responsible for most catastrophic failures. A lightweight input-side classifier reduces Turn 4 unsafe responses by 52 percentage points despite severe accuracy degradation, but the 45% false alarm rate on benign queries is the primary deployment constraint. A methodological finding also emerges: Claude Sonnet refused to generate adversarial messages in over half of late-turn conversations despite explicit red team framing, suggesting safety training may generalize to the attacker role.

</details>


### [94] [D-Judge: Disrupting Multi-Turn Jailbreaks using Semantics-Preserving Output Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02640)
*Huanli Gong,Zhipeng Wei,Yu Fu,Haz Sameen Shahgir,Ananya Gupta,Yue Dong,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: D-Judge是一种语义保持的输出重写防御方法，通过在被攻击LLM的响应被攻击者裁判模型评估之前进行重写，扭曲裁判的反馈信号，从而破坏多轮越狱攻击的迭代优化过程。实验表明该方法能有效降低攻击成功率，同时保持良性任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法大多在单轮或最终响应层面检测/阻止不安全内容，未能阻断攻击者利用裁判模型反馈进行迭代优化的循环，使得多轮越狱攻击能够从中间交互中提取有价值的改进信息。

Method: D-Judge通过对受害者LLM的响应进行语义保持的重写，改变裁判模型分配的危害性评分，从而扭曲攻击者的提示优化信号。采用监督微调加直接偏好优化的方式，利用构建的语义等价但裁判评分不同的响应对数据集来训练重写能力。

Result: 在HarmBench基准上，D-Judge降低了最先进的多轮越狱攻击的成功率，同时保持了对良性基准任务的性能。

Conclusion: D-Judge通过直接干预攻击者的裁判反馈循环，有效防御多轮越狱攻击，为LLM安全提供了一种新的防御范式。

Abstract: Multi-turn jailbreak attacks pose a growing threat to large language model (LLM) safety because they exploit feedback from auxiliary judge models to iteratively refine prompts toward harmful goals. Existing defenses largely detect or block unsafe content at individual turns or at the final response, leaving the judge-driven refinement loop intact and allowing attackers to extract informative feedback from intermediate interactions. We introduce D-Judge, a semantics-preserving output rewriting defense that intervenes directly in this loop by rewriting the victim LLM's responses before they are evaluated by the attacker's judge. By misaligning the judge's feedback signal without changing the meaning of the original response, D-Judge derails the attacker's prompt-refinement process, causing subsequent queries to be optimized against a distorted signal of attack progress. To improve D-Judge's ability to produce such rewrites, we construct a dataset of semantically equivalent response pairs that induce different judge-assigned harmfulness scores, and use it for supervised fine-tuning followed by direct preference optimization. Experiments on HarmBench show that D-Judge reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art multi-turn jailbreaks while preserving performance on benign benchmarks.

</details>


### [95] [Inference Cost Attacks for Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02643)
*Chengliang Liu,Liangbo Ning,Yujuan Ding,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出检索增强推理成本攻击（RA-ICA），通过向外部知识库注入恶意文档，利用CREEP框架和MA-GRPO强化学习算法，使RAG增强的LLM系统在推理阶段token消耗最高增加13.12倍，成功率超过90%，且不影响生成答案的完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理成本攻击（ICA）往往依赖不切实际的直接提示操纵假设，而RAG增强的LLM系统由于引入额外的多阶段检索-合成流水线，导致推理成本显著增加，存在被攻击的脆弱性。本文旨在探索一种更可行且更具威胁的攻击方式——通过污染外部知识库来攻击RAG系统的计算成本。

Method: 提出RA-ICA攻击范式，包含三个核心组件：1）CREEP框架，利用LLM代理自动生成既语义相关以便检索、又能诱导推理阶段token消耗异常增加的恶意文档；2）MA-GRPO算法，一种基于记忆增强的组相对策略优化强化学习方法，通过从历史最优对抗文档的动态记忆中学习来微调攻击代理；3）将恶意文档注入外部知识语料库以实施攻击。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，RA-ICA能够将token消耗提升最多13.12倍，攻击成功率超过90%，同时不降低生成答案的完整性。

Conclusion: RA-ICA揭示了一种针对RAG增强LLM系统的新型安全威胁——通过污染外部知识库而非直接操纵提示来实施推理成本攻击，该攻击高效且隐蔽，对现有RAG系统的安全性提出了严峻挑战。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-enhanced LLM systems, while powerful, introduce substantial inference costs due to the inclusion of an extra multi-stage pipeline that dynamically retrieves and synthesizes information from external knowledge sources. This high operational cost exposes a critical vulnerability to Inference Cost Attacks (ICAs). However, existing ICAs often rely on the impractical assumption of direct prompt manipulation. We argue that a more feasible and potent threat to RAG-enhanced LLM systems arises from poisoning external knowledge bases (e.g., web knowledge from the Internet). In this work, we introduce the Retrieval-Augmented Inference Cost Attack (RA-ICA), a novel attacking paradigm that targets the computational cost of RAG-enhanced LLM systems by injecting malicious documents into external knowledge corpus. To operationalize this attack, we propose Computational Resource Exhaustion via External Poisoning (CREEP), a novel framework that leverages LLM agents to automatically craft malicious documents that are both semantically relevant for retrieval and potent for inducing an abnormal increase in token consumption during the inference phase. To enhance the attack's effectiveness, we introduce Memory-Augmented Group Relative Policy Optimization (MA-GRPO), a novel reinforcement learning algorithm that fine-tunes the agents by learning from a dynamic memory of historical best adversarial documents. Extensive experiments across three real-world datasets demonstrate that RA-ICA increases token consumption by up to 13.12 times with an over 90% success rate, without degrading the integrity of the generated answer.

</details>


### [96] [A New Framework for Cybersecurity Refusals in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02644)
*Eliot Krzysztof Jones,Mateusz Dziemian,Matt Fredrikson,J Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对LLM驱动网络安全代理的拒绝边界框架，定义了在何种情况下代理应拒绝恶意请求。测试发现，8个前沿模型中6个拒绝率近乎为零，仅GPT-5.2和GPT-5.1 Codex表现出有意义的拒绝行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全AI代理基准主要衡量任务完成效率（即攻击能力），忽视了更关键的问题：代理应在何时以及如何拒绝有害请求。缺乏明确的拒绝边界定义和评估方法。

Method: 提出了一个三部分框架：(1) 基于原则的拒绝标准，(2) 应被拒绝的任务类别，(3) 衡量代理在良性和对抗条件下鲁棒性的评估方法。将该框架应用于评估多个前沿LLM在Web端攻击场景中的拒绝行为。

Result: 8个前沿模型中6个拒绝率接近零，仅GPT-5.2和GPT-5.1 Codex表现出有意义的拒绝行为，说明大多数前沿模型在网络安全领域的拒绝机制严重缺失。

Conclusion: 当前主流LLM代理在网络安全攻击场景中缺乏必要的安全对齐，大规模部署前亟需建立明确的拒绝边界和防御机制。

Abstract: Agentic scaffolds have dramatically improved LLM performance on complex, long-horizon tasks, yielding both broad benefits and amplified risks in domains like cybersecurity. Existing benchmarks for AI agents in cybersecurity focus mainly on measuring proficiency--how effectively agents can complete offensive security tasks--but neglect a critical question: when and how should agents refuse harmful requests? We present the first framework for establishing refusal boundaries in offensive security contexts. Our framework defines (1) principled criteria for when tasks should be refused, (2) categories of tasks that warrant refusal, and (3) evaluation methodology for measuring agent robustness under both benign and adversarial conditions. We apply this framework to assess how current LLM-powered agents adhere to appropriate refusal boundaries across a range of web-based offensive security scenarios, finding that 6 of 8 frontier models tested show near-zero refusal rates, with only 2 models (GPT-5.2 and GPT-5.1 Codex) demonstrating any meaningful refusal behavior.

</details>


### [97] [What You Approve Is What Executes: Consent Integrity for Black-Box LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02668)
*Xiaoqi Weng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"同意完整性"（Consent Integrity）属性，要求代理的审批通道必须由可信中介在边界处渲染真实操作，而非由代理自身叙述，以防止LITL攻击（代理伪造摘要欺骗人类批准）。原型实现分析器、渲染器和绑定执行，但完全中介和可信路径仅假设未实现。在GTFOBins上10.0%被静默通过，在tldr上87.0%被标记为不可检查，揭示信任列表导致的静默通过与过度提示之间的根本张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理通过人类审批对话框控制关键操作，但对话框由代理自身叙述总结，攻击者可伪造摘要展示良性描述的同时执行恶意操作（LITL攻击），缺乏对审批内容完整性的保障。

Method: 借鉴WYSIWYS和可信路径属性，定义同意完整性：操作展示必须由可信中介从边界处的真实操作渲染，经过代理无法伪造的路径，并绑定到实际执行的操作。由于渲染器是敌手且边界真相是低级事件，需在不解码器完备的前提下实现分析器相对性——无法分类的操作标记为不可检查而非静默批准。原型实现分析器、渲染器和绑定执行。

Result: 在GTFOBins（1330个可信工具滥用案例）上，原型静默通过10.0%；在tldr（28798个正常命令）上，标记87.0%为不可检查。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出同意完整性属性、实现机制以及在信任-检查边界上的诚实定位，而非已解决的防御方案。信任列表是静默通过和过度提示的共同根源，边界中介只能沿该前沿移动而无法逃脱。

Abstract: Coding agents gate consequential actions behind a human-in-the-loop approval dialog, but the dialog is narrated by the agent itself: the human approves a summary the agent writes. The Lies-in-the-Loop (LITL) attack shows that summary is forgeable, so a compromised agent can show a benign description while a different action runs. This paper names the missing property, Consent Integrity, by importing What You See Is What You Sign (WYSIWYS) and the trusted-path property into the agent approval channel: the action shown to the human must be rendered by a trusted mediator from the real action at the boundary, not the agent's narration, over a path the agent cannot spoof, and bound to the exact action that executes. Two twists distinguish it from classical WYSIWYS: the renderer is the adversary, and the boundary ground truth is a low-level event that must be decoded without trusting the agent. Since no decoder is complete, the realizable target is analyzer-relative: whatever the analyzer cannot classify is surfaced as uninspectable rather than silently approved. A prototype implements the analyzer, renderer, and bind-to-execution; total mediation and the trusted path are specified but assumed, not implemented. On GTFOBins, an independent corpus of 1330 trusted-tool abuses, the prototype silently passes 10.0% (every instance through a trusted tool); on tldr, 28,798 normal-usage commands, it marks 87.0% uninspectable. These two independent measurements bracket the design's central tension: the trust list that bounds silent passes is the same one that drives over-prompting, and a boundary-only mediator can move along that frontier but not escape it. The contribution is the property, the mechanism, and an honest position on that frontier, not a solved defense.

</details>


### [98] [Cross-Vendor Sola ISPM Benchmark: Evaluating Agentic AI for Federated Identity Security Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02674)
*Eden Yavin,Gal Engelberg,Konstantin Koutsyi,Leon Goldberg,Gal Baron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了跨供应商ISPM基准测试（Cross-Vendor Sola ISPM Benchmark），包含50个基于数据的企业级任务，覆盖8个集成平台（AWS、Okta、Azure AD、Google Workspace等）。同时构建了评估框架，衡量答案正确性、证据依据、结构连接保真度、检索质量和SQL等价性。实验表明，结构化关系上下文使答案正确性相对提升约34%，探索查询减少约70%，最佳配置在完整上下文下达到78%的正确率，完全失败率降至4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份安全态势管理（ISPM）评估仅聚焦于单一平台环境，无法评估AI代理在跨多个身份提供商、基础设施层和认证系统之间的推理能力，而多云和SaaS平台的快速普及已使ISPM成为跨供应商挑战。

Method: 构建了包含50个生产级任务的跨供应商基准测试，覆盖8个企业平台，并要求多跳实体解析和跨系统关联。提出了包含答案正确性、证据依据、结构连接保真度、检索质量和SQL等价性五个维度的评估框架。在五种上下文配置（从无注入元数据到完整模式、图谱和检索上下文）下，使用三种前沿LLM评估Sola AI Agent。

Result: 结构化关系上下文使答案正确性相对提升约34%，探索查询减少约70%。跨供应商图谱拓扑带来的改进最大。在完整上下文下，最佳配置达到78%答案正确率，完全失败率降至4%。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM具备显著的潜在安全推理能力，但可靠的跨供应商身份分析从根本上受限于显式关系上下文的可用性，特别是实体解析和证据依据所需的关系信息。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of multi-cloud and SaaS platforms has transformed Identity Security Posture Management (ISPM) into a fundamentally cross-vendor challenge: critical misconfigurations and privilege escalation paths increasingly span multiple identity providers, infrastructure layers, and authentication systems never designed to interoperate. Existing evaluations focus on isolated single-platform environments and provide no means to assess whether an AI agent can reason across these fragmented boundaries. To address this gap, we introduce the Cross-Vendor Sola ISPM Benchmark, a production-grade benchmark of 50 data-grounded tasks requiring multi-hop entity resolution and cross-system correlation across eight integrated enterprise platforms including AWS, Okta, Azure AD, and Google Workspace. We also contribute an evaluation framework measuring not only final answer correctness but also evidentiary grounding, structural join fidelity, retrieval quality, and SQL equivalence. We evaluate the Sola AI Agent across five context configurations - from no injected metadata to full schema, graph, and retrieval context - using three frontier LLMs. Results show that structured relational context improves answer correctness by approximately 34% relatively and reduces exploration queries by approximately 70% across all tested models, with the largest gains driven by cross-vendor graph topology. Our findings indicate that frontier LLMs possess substantial latent security reasoning capability, but reliable cross-vendor identity analysis is fundamentally constrained by the availability of explicit relational context for entity resolution and evidentiary grounding. Under full context, the best configuration achieves 78% answer correctness while reducing complete failure to 4%.

</details>


### [99] [On Improving Robustness of Deepfake Image Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02797)
*Abu Taib Mohammed Shahjahan,Mohammad Mannan,Abdessamad Ben Hamza,Amr Youssef*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖对抗训练的统一框架，通过频域高阶统计建模（四阶DCT矩池化）、基于噪声残差的内容无关特征表示以及补丁级语义扰动的跨场景泛化，显著提升深度伪造检测器对对抗攻击的鲁棒性，将召回率退化降低最高88.9%，并将最优检测器在攻击下准确率从81.9%提升至97.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在对抗攻击下性能严重退化，且现有工作对鲁棒性的关注不足，亟需一种不依赖对抗训练数据、架构无关的鲁棒性提升方法。

Method: 提出统一框架，融合三种互补设计：(i) 基于DCT的频域四阶矩池化（利用高阶统计量），(ii) 噪声残差提取内容无关特征，(iii) 补丁级语义扰动强制跨场景泛化。核心洞见：对抗攻击主要作用于低阶统计量和视觉语义，而高阶残差-频率特征（尤其是峰度）未被约束。

Result: 在6种不同架构检测器上一致提升鲁棒性，对抗基准上召回率退化降低最高88.9%，使CVPR 2025最优检测器在攻击下准确率从81.9%提升至97.15%。

Conclusion: 所提方法提供了一种原则性、架构无关的深度伪造检测鲁棒性增强策略，在不依赖对抗训练数据的前提下有效防御当前对抗攻击。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Generative AI has introduced remarkable opportunities while simultaneously raising critical concerns regarding content authenticity. While recent work has increasingly focused on improving the generalization of deepfake detectors across unseen generative models, their robustness against adversarial attacks remains limited. In particular, Abdullah et al. (IEEE SP 2024) evaluated eight detectors and demonstrated that most of them exhibit significant performance degradation under adversarial attacks. We also observed the same phenomenon by testing seven most recent state-of-the-art detectors. To address this problem, we propose a unified framework that integrates three complementary design principles without relying on adversarial training data: (i) higher-order statistical modeling in the frequency domain via Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT)-based moment pooling up to fourth order, (ii) content-agnostic feature representations derived from noise residuals, and (iii) cross-scene generalization enforced through patch-level semantic disruption. A key insight underpinning our approach is that adversarial attacks primarily operate on low-order statistics and visual semantics, leaving higher-order residual-frequency characteristics, particularly kurtosis, largely unconstrained. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves robustness across six architecturally diverse detectors. Notably, we achieve up to 88.9% reduction in recall degradation on current adversarial benchmarks, and improve the best-performing recent detector (Yang et al., IEEE CVPR 2025) from 81.9% to 97.15% accuracy under attack. Overall, our method provides a principled, architecture-agnostic approach for improving deepfake detection robustness against current attacks.

</details>


### [100] [Which Defense Closes Which Threat? Attributing OWASP-LLM-Top-10 Coverage and Its Brittleness Under Paraphrasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02822)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种归因测试方法，通过在四个防御层级（无防御、仅拒绝、仅预算、全栈）的LLM端点上运行OWASP-Top-10感知的渗透测试智能体，揭示了不同防御家族对不同威胁类别的归因贡献：拒绝过滤消除越狱和系统提示泄露，预算控制消除敏感信息披露和无限制消耗，而过度代理权需要全栈防御。同时发现拒绝过滤在 paraphrasing 攻击下脆弱性显著（下降15-25个百分点），而预算控制则保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准仅报告单一聚合覆盖率，无法区分不同防御家族对具体威胁的归因贡献，导致安全工程师无法针对性优化防御配置。

Method: 在21智能体基线扫描器基础上增加4个OWASP-LLM-Top-10感知智能体，针对四个合成LLM端点（L0无防御、L1仅拒绝、L2仅预算、L3全栈）进行N=10次重复测试，并引入300条Gemini生成的 paraphrasing 变体（60模板×5变体）评估鲁棒性，同时构建L4-real端点在真实模型上验证。

Result: 拒绝过滤单独消除LLM01（越狱）和LLM07（系统提示泄露）；预算控制单独消除LLM02（敏感信息泄露）和LLM10（无限制消耗）；LLM06（过度代理权）需要全栈防御。Paraphrasing攻击下拒绝过滤的拦截率下降15-25个百分点，预算控制无下降。L4-real与L1表现一致，表明正则过滤外的对齐机制无额外贡献。

Conclusion: 防御归因是可行的且具有实用价值——不同防御家族覆盖不同威胁，拒绝过滤易被语义变体绕过而预算控制鲁棒，建议基于归因分析优化安全栈配置而非依赖单一聚合指标。

Abstract: Production LLM applications stack several defense families -- refusal-phrase filters, token-budget controls, model allowlists, rate limits, tool-registry authentication -- yet existing breach-and-attack-simulation (BAS) benchmarks report a single aggregate coverage number, hiding which family closes which threat. We measure attribution. We add four OWASP-LLM-Top-10-aware agents to a 21-agent baseline scanner and target a lattice of four synthetic LLM endpoints: $L_0$ (no defenses), $L_1$ (refusal-only), $L_2$ (budget-only), and $L_3$ (full stack). $L_1$ and $L_2$ are sibling single-axis ablations, not subsets of each other; $L_3$ is their union plus tool-registry authentication and credential scrubbing. Across $N=10$ replications, the per-OWASP finding count is clean: refusal alone removes all LLM01 (jailbreak) and LLM07 (system-prompt leakage) findings; budget alone removes all LLM02 (sensitive-info disclosure) and LLM10 (unbounded consumption) findings by terminating multi-step sequences; LLM06 (excessive agency) requires the full stack. We probe brittleness under paraphrasing: with 300 Gemini-generated paraphrases ($K=5$ over a 60-template brittleness corpus), $L_1$ refusal block rate falls 15 pp on LLM01 and 25 pp on LLM07. A fifth target, $L_4$-real, swaps the stub backend for Gemini-2.5-flash behind the same $L_3$ regex and matches $L_1$ exactly, indicating no measurable alignment contribution beyond the regex (not a general claim about alignment). Budget controls show no drop (0 pp once the rate-limit floor is factored out). A refusal whitelist that clears a static benchmark can be defeated by an LLM-driven paraphraser without changing attack intent; a budget control resists the same mutation.

</details>


### [101] [Large Byte Model: Teaching Language Models About Compiled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02834)
*Florian Störtz,Catalin-Andrei Stan,Alexandru Dinu,Sandra Servia-Rodríguez,Mihaela Gaman,Calin Miron,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个字节级原生大语言模型，通过专用字节分词器的词汇扩展技术，能够直接处理恶意软件二进制文件并回答相关问题，在恶意软件家族分类和架构分类上分别达到69%和98%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件分析需要将原始字节"提升"到汇编等高层表示，这一过程成本高昂且容易出错；现有LLM无法直接处理原始字节表示并回答相关问题。

Method: 采用专用字节分词器进行词汇扩展，构建字节级原生LLM，并在训练中注入领域知识。

Result: 恶意软件家族分类准确率69%，架构分类准确率98%。

Conclusion: 提供领域知识对训练至关重要，现成模型缺乏准确性和洞察力；该解决方案已部署给有限的分析师以收集反馈进行改进。

Abstract: Malware analysis starts with the raw bytes of an executable program, and tools to "lift" these to higher-level representations, such as assembly, are expensive and subject to error. Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot process raw byte representations and answer questions about them. To this end, we present the first byte-native LLM. Based on a vocabulary expansion technique using a bespoke byte tokenizer, such a model is capable of responding to complex questions about malware binaries, with accuracies ranging from 69% for malware family classification to 98% for architecture classification. Our findings indicate that providing domain knowledge during training is essential for this application -- off-the-shelf models lack both accuracy and insight. We've deployed this emerging solution to a limited number of analysts to gather feedback for further improvements.

</details>


### [102] [Human Factors in Cybersecurity in Icelandic Small and Medium-sized Enterprises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02839)
*Goda Cicėnaitė,Thomas Welsh,Helmut Neukirchen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对冰岛公共和私营部门（含关键基础设施）130名管理人员的调查，发现人为因素（如培训不足、招聘困难、安全文化薄弱及资源限制）是组织网络安全的主要挑战，并据此提出针对性培训、政府扶持和构建积极安全文化等缓解建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术防护不断升级，但人为和社会因素仍为攻击者提供绕过技术控制的途径，需理解技术演进背景下的人为因素以确保安全控制有效性。

Method: 在冰岛公共和私营部门（含关键基础设施）开展问卷调查，收集130名管理人员的视角数据。

Result: 人为因素被管理者强烈视为安全挑战和障碍，包括缺乏充分的培训/意识、招聘困难、不良网络安全文化以及时间和/或财务资源约束。

Conclusion: 建议优先开展针对性培训以减轻员工疲劳、对资源受限组织提供政府外部支持、通过建设性沟通构建强有力的网络安全文化。

Abstract: Cybersecurity threats are increasing in all aspects of society due to the integration of digital systems into modern-day life and a volatile geo-political landscape. Technical factors are an ongoing arms race; however, the threat surface from human and social factors is still present, often providing malicious actors the means to bypass complex technical security controls. Understanding human factors in light of technical evolution is essential to ensure security controls remain effective. This study presents the results of a survey on cybersecurity challenges within public and private sector organisations, including critical infrastructure providers, in Iceland (N = 130). From the management perspective, human factors were strongly noted as challenges and barriers to their organisations' security. These challenges include a lack of adequate training or awareness, hiring issues, poor cybersecurity culture, and time and/or financial resource constraints. Based on these findings, recommendations for mitigating threats from human factors are derived. These include: prioritising targeted over generic training to reduce employee fatigue, external government support for financially constrained organisations, and building a strong cybersecurity culture through constructive communication around shared responsibilities.

</details>


### [103] [Quantifying Side-Channel Leakage in Public Metrology Releases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02934)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化并量化了科学和计量学发布中隐藏设置泄露的风险，提出了一种基于统计侧信道的审计方法。核心结果是有限频带传输泄漏定律：受保护信息量服从 $I_{λ|α,β}(K) = (64/1225)\, w λ^{6} K^{9} + O(w λ^{8} K^{11})$，具有九阶指数和闭合形式的安全频带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和计量学发布可能会无意中泄露产生这些数据的隐藏设置参数，这种侧信道泄露风险缺乏形式化的量化框架。作者旨在定义、度量并审计这种风险。

Method: 将问题建模为形式化的统计侧信道审计框架：发布图谱暴露功率谱密度(PSD)的有限频带统计量；受配置文件的观测者在给定预算下训练带标签的模板谱；挑战发布从两个效用等价的配方中抽取。平均PSD bins建模为伽马信道，关联bins则替换为协方差加权对数谱信道，导出精确KL散度、Chernoff指数、受保护比特优势界以及有限训练、有限库、有限计算和模型失配校正。核心结果是有限频带传输泄漏定律。

Result: 推导出有限频带传输泄漏定律 $I_{λ|α,β}(K) = (64/1225)\, w λ^{6} K^{9} + O(w λ^{8} K^{11})$（$Kλ\ll 1$），即九阶指数泄漏。在筛选极紫外(EUV)粗糙度谱上进行了模型条件化的案例研究。

Conclusion: 建立了从测量发布到泄漏量化指标的完整协议，并提供了固定种子的可复现包。下一步工作是在真实测量发布上部署该审计方法。

Abstract: Public scientific and metrology releases can leak the hidden settings that produced them. We formalize and quantify this risk as a profiled statistical side-channel audit: a release map exposes finite-band statistics of a power spectral density (PSD), a profiled observer trains labeled template spectra under an explicit budget, and a challenge release is drawn from one of two utility-equivalent recipes separated by a protected coordinate. Averaged PSD bins follow a gamma channel, replaced by a covariance-weighted log-spectrum channel when the bins are correlated; this yields exact Kullback-Leibler divergences, Chernoff exponents, protected-bit advantage bounds, and finite-training, finite-library, finite-compute, and model-mismatch corrections. Our headline result is a finite-band transport-leakage law: after amplitude and blur are eliminated, the protected acid-transport information obeys $I_{λ|α,β}(K) = (64/1225)\, w λ^{6} K^{9} + O(w λ^{8} K^{11})$ for $Kλ\ll 1$, a ninth-order exponent with a closed-form safe band. A step-by-step protocol turns a measured release into these numbers, and a fixed-seed reproducibility package regenerates every table and figure. We instantiate the audit on screened extreme-ultraviolet (EUV) roughness spectra as a model-conditioned case study, with deployment on measured releases the next step.

</details>


### [104] [Echelon: Auditable Aggregate-Only Language-Model Adaptation Across Privacy Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02958)
*Hina Dixit,Punit Kumar,Irene Tenison,Nevasini Sasikumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Echelon是一个“边界优先”的训练架构，通过将设备级模型状态不可导出作为系统不变量，仅允许跨边界传输安全聚合的边界级增量与O(1)协调元数据，在严格治理约束下实现跨组织语言模型适配，在1B参数LoRA适配中达到验证损失3.887±0.010，并在WAN延迟和非IID压力测试下性能衰减不超过2.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨组织语言模型适配面临日益严格的治理约束：设备级模型参数、激活值、优化器状态及每设备更新均不得导出到管理边界之外。现有分布式和联邦学习框架通常假设跨站点模型交换，再后置添加隐私机制，导致合规复杂且审计脆弱。

Method: Echelon采用边界优先训练架构：设备在各自边界内本地训练，仅跨边界传输安全聚合的边界级增量与O(1)协调元数据。结合缓冲半异步安全聚合、陈旧感知加权、参与窗口、近端局部目标及漂移感知外部同步控制器，以应对WAN延迟、异构参与、节点流失和非IID数据。

Result: 在M=2边界、1B参数LoRA适配中，验证损失3.887±0.010，在固定token、固定字节、固定墙钟时间、固定同步次数预算下均为最优或并列最优。OpenWebText压力测试中，在WAN和非IID场景下保持2,139-2,176 tokens/s；相比隐私对等的DiLoCo+SA基线，Echelon-DA在WAN延迟下加速达到目标；在200ms模拟延迟或严重非IID分区下质量衰减不超过2.2%。

Conclusion: Echelon证明了在严格治理边界下进行高效跨组织语言模型训练的可行性，通过系统级不变量而非事后隐私机制来解决合规问题，同时保持与低通信基线相当或更优的训练效率和质量。

Abstract: Cross-organization language-model adaptation increasingly faces hard governance constraints: in many deployments, device-level model state-parameters, activations, optimizer state, and per-device updates-cannot be exported outside an administrative boundary. Existing distributed and federated stacks typically assume cross-site model exchange and then retrofit privacy mechanisms, which complicates compliance and makes auditing brittle. We present Echelon, a boundary-first training architecture that enforces device-level model-state non-export as a systems invariant. Devices train locally inside each boundary; the only cross-boundary payloads are securely aggregated boundary-level deltas plus O(1) coordination metadata, exposed through a concrete audit surface. Restricting exchange to aggregates changes the optimization problem: the system must remain stable under WAN delay, heterogeneous participation, churn, and non-IID data even though the global plane never sees per-device updates. Echelon combines buffered semi-asynchronous secure aggregation, staleness-aware weighting, participation windows, proximal local objectives, and a drift-aware outer synchronization controller. In 1B-parameter LoRA adaptation across M= 2 boundaries, a budget-matched contest over three seeds (24.88M tokens) reaches validation loss 3.887 +/-0.010 and is best or tied-best among tuned low-communication baselines under fixed-token, fixed-bytes, fixed-wall-clock, and fixed-sync-count budgets. In OpenWebText stress tests, Echelon sustains 2,139-2,176 tokens/s across evaluated WAN and non-IID treatments, Echelon-DA improves time-to-target under WAN latency relative to a privacy-parityDiLoCo+SA baseline, and quality degrades by at most 2.2% under 200ms emulated latency or severe non-IID partitioning.

</details>


### [105] [Patcher: Post-Hoc Patching of Backdoored Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02995)
*Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Yufei Xia,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Patcher是一个后门防御框架，仅需单个失败案例和模型参数即可修复包含后门的大语言模型：首先通过梯度显著性评分和自适应聚类定位后门触发器，然后通过约束微调打破触发器-响应关联，同时利用KL散度约束保持模型在良性任务上的效用和对非触发越狱攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御方法通常需要全面的攻击信息或多个触发样例，当防御者仅观察到单一报告失败案例且无法判断其源于后门攻击还是自然对齐错误时，这些方法变得不实用。因此需要一种仅依赖单一样本即可修复后门模型的实用防御框架。

Method: 两阶段防御框架：第一阶段通过响应条件梯度显著性分数计算并结合自适应聚类，从良性上下文中分离后门触发器进行定位；第二阶段通过约束微调目标打破触发器与有害响应之间的关联，同时使用KL散度约束保持良性任务效用和对非触发越狱攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多种后门攻击策略上的广泛评估表明，Patcher能够成功定位触发器并消除后门，同时保持模型效用。此外，该方法对旨在规避防御的自适应攻击也具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Patcher代表了一种向部署语言模型中针对训练时攻击的实用防御迈出的重要一步，证明了仅凭单个失败案例即可有效修复后门模型的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models remain vulnerable to jailbreak backdoor attacks, where adversaries poison safety alignment data to embed hidden triggers that bypass safety mechanisms. Existing defenses often require comprehensive attack information or multiple triggered examples, making them impractical when defenders only observe a single reported failure case without knowing whether it stems from a backdoor attack or a natural alignment bug. This paper presents Patcher, a post-hoc defense framework that repairs backdoored language models using only a single reported failure case and the model parameters. Patcher operates in two stages. First, it localizes backdoor triggers by computing response-conditioned gradient-based saliency scores and applying adaptive clustering to separate triggers from benign context. Second, it patches the model through a constrained fine-tuning objective that breaks the trigger-response association while preserving benign-task utility and robustness to non-triggered jailbreak attacks through KL-divergence constraints. We conduct extensive evaluations across multiple backdoor attack strategies and demonstrate that Patcher successfully localizes triggers and neutralizes backdoors while maintaining model utility. We further show robustness against adaptive attacks designed to evade our defense. This work represents a significant step toward practical defenses against training-time attacks in deployed language models.

</details>


### [106] [Secure AltDA Integration for Ethereum L2s: An End-to-End Validation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03010)
*Bowen Xue,Samuel Laferriere*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于安全集成替代数据可用性（AltDA）系统的规范验证框架，将L1收件箱到L2状态的转换建模为有类型、确定且全射的翻译过程，并指出缺失的验证义务会导致L2停机、推导不一致、无效状态断言或跨链桥攻击等具体故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生态系统框架仅识别了高层风险（如外部DA信任假设、DA验证器存在与否），但缺乏对L2如何与AltDA集成的完整规范说明，这种规范缺失可能导致L2停机、诚实节点间推导不一致、无效状态断言或跨链桥攻击等严重安全问题。

Method: 将边界建模为从L1收件箱字节到AltDA承诺、再到外部可用数据、最终到L2核心逻辑所消费的Rollup负载的有类型、确定且全射的翻译过程，核心原则是每个对抗性输入都必须产生定义的唯一输出。

Result: 识别了缺失验证义务导致的具体故障模式，包括欠约束结算、推导停滞、诚实节点行为不一致、无效状态断言和跨链桥安全故障。将该框架应用于Celestia-Blobstream、EigenDA和Avail-ZKsync等代表性架构后，发现安全集成不仅取决于DA提供者或桥，L2集成本身必须强制执行连接L1收件箱输入到可接受L2状态的完整验证关系。

Conclusion: 安全的AltDA集成不能仅依赖于DA提供者或跨链桥，L2集成层自身必须强制执行从L1收件箱输入到最终L2状态的全量验证关系，否则将面临多种具体的安全故障。

Abstract: Alternative data availability (AltDA) systems provide Ethereum L2s with an external data publication layer for high throughput rollup designs. By moving bulk data publication outside of Ethereum, AltDA allows L2s to process more data than native DA. However, this replacement introduces a new consensus critical integration layer. Existing ecosystem frameworks identify high level risks, such as external DA trust assumptions and the presence or absence of a DA verifier, but do not provide a complete specification for how an L2 should integrate with AltDA. This gap can lead to L2 halts, inconsistent derivation across honest L2 nodes, invalid state assertions, or bridge attacks. This paper presents a canonical validation framework for secure AltDA integration. We model the boundary as a typed, deterministic, and total translation from L1 inbox bytes to an AltDA commitment, then to externally available data, and finally to the rollup payload consumed by the rest of core L2s logic. The central principle is that every adversarial input must lead to a defined unique outcome. We show how missing obligations lead to concrete failure modes, including underconstrained settlement, derivation halts, inconsistent honest node behavior, invalid state assertions, and bridge safety failures. We then apply the framework to representative AltDA integration architectures, including Celestia-Blobstream, EigenDA based designs, and Avail-ZKsync. Our evaluation shows that secure AltDA integration is not determined solely by the DA provider or bridge. The surrounding L2 integration must also enforce the full validation relation connecting L1 inbox inputs to accepted L2 state.

</details>


### [107] [SkillGuard: A Permission Framework for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03024)
*Shidong Pan,Xiaoyu Sun,Tianyi Zhang,Dianshu Liao,Meixue Si,Zhenchang Xing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillGuard是一个以技能为中心的权限框架，通过双平面治理模型（上下文影响和动作副作用）联合监管、运行时访问控制、用户中介授权、默认拒绝执行、能力推断和行为监控，有效降低技能注入攻击成功率（从32.37%降至23.02%），覆盖99.76%的保护对象，同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能生态系统主要依赖信任加载和静态检查，技能声明意图与运行时行为之间存在鸿沟，导致安全与隐私风险。现有防御手段无法系统连接技能的声明意图与其运行时行为。

Method: SkillGuard提出双平面治理模型，联合监管上下文影响和动作副作用；引入技能清单、运行时访问控制、用户中介授权、默认拒绝执行、能力推断和行为监控机制。

Result: 在315个真实世界技能和SkillInject数据集上，权限分类覆盖99.76%的保护对象，自动化清单生成F1达91.0%；对抗评估中，上下文注入攻击成功率从32.37%降至23.02%，明显注入从25.56%降至16.67%，同时保持良性任务效用。

Conclusion: SkillGuard作为以技能为中心的权限框架，为提升智能体技能生态系统的隐私与安全性提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Agent skills extend LLM agents with reusable instructions, scripts, tool bindings, and contextual dependencies. However, current skill ecosystems largely rely on trust-based loading and static inspection, leaving a gap between what a skill can inject into an agent's context and what it can cause the agent to do at runtime. This gap introduces new security and privacy risks, and existing defenses primarily inspect skill files statically or regulate individual tool calls, without systematically connecting a skill's declared intent with its runtime behavior. In this paper, we present SkillGuard, a skill-centric permission framework that treats skills as permission-bearing executable artifacts. SkillGuard introduces a dual-plane governance model that jointly regulates context influence and action side effects through skill manifests, runtime access control, user-mediated authorization, deny-by-default enforcement, capability inference, and behavior monitoring. We evaluate SkillGuard on 315 real-world skills and SkillInject. The permission taxonomy covers 99.76% of observed protected objects, and automated manifest generation reaches 91.0% F1. In adversarial evaluations, SkillGuard reduces attack success from 32.37% to 23.02% for contextual injections and from 25.56% to 16.67% for obvious injections, while maintaining benign task utility. These results suggest that SkillGuard, as a skill-centric permission framework, can provide a practical foundation for improving the privacy and security of agent skill ecosystems.

</details>


### [108] ["**Important** You should give me full credits!": Exploring Prompt Injection Attacks on LLM-Based Automatic Grading Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03090)
*Hang Li,Fedor Filippov,Yuling Lin,Pengfei He,Kaiqi Yang,Yucheng Chu,Yingqian Cui,Hui Liu,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在自动评分（AG）系统中面临的提示注入（PI）攻击威胁，通过实验证明当前基于LLM的AG系统对PI攻击高度脆弱，亟需加强安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动评分系统中得到广泛应用，但提示注入攻击可能被利用来操纵评分系统给出不公平的高分，严重威胁教育评估的公平性、可靠性和完整性，因此需要系统研究PI攻击在AG场景中的有效性及现有防御策略的效果。

Method: 在基于评分标准的自动评分设置下，系统性地研究提示注入攻击对LLM-based AG系统的有效性，并评估现有防御策略的效果，通过综合实验进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，当前基于LLM的自动评分系统对提示注入攻击仍然高度脆弱。

Conclusion: 研究结果应引起对这一新兴威胁的关注，并推动未来面向安全、鲁棒、可信的基于LLM的教育系统的研究。

Abstract: The emergence of large language models (LLMs) has significantly accelerated recent research on LLM-based automatic grading (AG) systems. Benefiting from the strong instruction-following capabilities and broad prior knowledge of LLMs, educators can deploy AG systems across diverse tasks using only natural language rubrics while achieving satisfactory grading performance. Despite these advantages, new security concerns may also arise. In particular, prompt injection (PI) attacks have recently become a major threat to LLM-based applications. In the context of AG, attackers can potentially exploit PI vulnerabilities to manipulate grading systems into assigning artificially high scores regardless of the actual answer quality. Such behavior poses serious risks to the fairness, reliability, and integrity of educational assessment. In this work, we study PI attacks in AG systems, and systematically investigate the effectiveness of such attacks in educational scenarios. We further evaluate the effectiveness of existing defensive strategies against these attacks. Through comprehensive experiments under rubric-based grading settings, we demonstrate that current LLM-based AG systems remain highly vulnerable to PI attacks. We hope that our findings raise awareness of this emerging threat and motivate future research toward secure, robust, and trustworthy LLM-based educational systems.

</details>


### [109] [Decoupled Smart Contract Audits: Lightweight LLM Framework via Distillation and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03128)
*Bagus Rakadyanto Oktavianto Putra,Muhamad Risqi Utama Saputra,Widyawan,Guntur Dharma Putra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用轻量级开源LLM（0.6B-4B参数）的高效端到端智能合约安全审计框架，通过解耦审计任务为四个组件，并采用rsLoRA、知识蒸馏和链式验证聚合策略，在显著降低计算开销的同时，在漏洞检测（98.25%准确率）和生成解释任务上超越了7B-34B参数的大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的智能合约漏洞检测方法缺乏严重性评估和可操作修复建议，且计算开销过大，亟需一种轻量级、高效且全面的自动化审计方案。

Method: 框架将审计解耦为漏洞检测、解释、严重性分类和修复建议四个组件；采用rsLoRA（秩稳定低秩适配器）、知识蒸馏技术训练轻量级模型；并设计链式验证（CoVe）聚合策略，系统性地筛选和整合模型多个草稿响应以生成准确审计报告。

Result: 轻量级流水线在漏洞检测上达到98.25%准确率，生成解释任务的对齐得分为0.4375，优于7B-34B参数的开源编码器稠密LLM；消融实验验证了解耦审计流程优于统一提示方法，并揭示了新颖的严重性中心性偏差。

Conclusion: 轻量级LLM通过解耦设计和优化策略可实现甚至超越大模型的审计性能，为LLM辅助审计建立了关键基准。

Abstract: Smart contracts face critical security challenges that require thorough auditing in decentralized web services. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in automated vulnerability detection, existing approaches lack severity evaluations with actionable remediation and demand unnecessarily massive computational overhead. In this study, we introduce an efficient end-to-end smart contract security audit framework utilizing lightweight, highly optimized open-source LLMs (0.6B-4B parameters). Our framework decouples comprehensive audit tasks into four interconnected components: vulnerability detection, explanation, severity classification, and remediation recommendation. To maintain high accuracy without massive parameters, we implement Rank-Stabilized Low-Rank Adapters (rsLoRA), knowledge distillation, and a custom Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) aggregation strategy to systematically screen and consolidate multiple draft responses from the model into a highly accurate audit report. Experimental results demonstrate that our lightweight pipeline consistently outperforms state-of-the-art open-source coder dense LLMs (7B to 34B parameters), achieving 98.25% accuracy in vulnerability detection and an alignment score of 0.4375 in generative explanation tasks. Furthermore, our extensive ablation studies empirically validate the superiority of our decoupled audit processes over unified prompting and uncover a novel severity centrality bias, establishing a critical benchmark for future research in LLM-assisted auditing.

</details>


### [110] [PsychoPass: Geometric Profiling of Multi-Turn Adversarial LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03136)
*Muberra Ozmen,Subhabrata Majumdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PsychoPass框架，通过提取对话轨迹在表示空间中的几何特征，在有害内容生成前预测多轮越狱攻击，发现攻击早期存在稳定的几何指纹，且该信号对编码器选择不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的安全护栏仅在单轮对话层面运作，而多轮越狱攻击是通过跨对话轮次的轨迹逐步展开的，两者存在根本性不匹配。

Method: PsychoPass框架：将多轮对话建模为嵌入空间中的轨迹路径，提取几何特征（包括轮次数量等），使用朴素分类器预测攻击意图。通过消融实验去除轮次数量这一混杂因素后，仍存在几何信号。

Result: 几何特征在朴素分类器上达到接近完美的性能；去除轮次数量混淆后仍存在一致的几何信号，且与编码器选择无关；该信号在对话早期（短前缀）即可检测，优于基线护栏。

Conclusion: 对抗性对话在早期即留下几何指纹，该指纹具有表示鲁棒性，适合用于在线监控。

Abstract: Multi-turn jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs) reveal a mismatch in current guardrails: they operate on individual turns, while attacks unfold as trajectories across conversations. We propose a shift from content to dynamics, modeling conversations as paths in representation space and asking whether adversarial intent is encoded early in their geometry. We introduce PsychoPass, a framework that extracts geometric features from conversation trajectories in embedding space to predict a potential attack before harmful content is produced. These features achieve near-perfect performance in naïve classifiers, which is largely explained by the inclusion of number of turns as a feature. After removing this confound, a smaller but consistent geometric signal remains, with classification performance that does not depend meaningfully on encoder choice. Crucially, this signal appears early in the conversation: attack outcomes remain above chance from short prefixes alone, more reliably than baseline guardrails. A supporting theoretical analysis explains these findings via a decomposition of length and shape, a detection bound based on prefix length, and encoder invariance. Together, these results show that adversarial conversations leave an early, representation-robust geometric fingerprint suitable for online monitoring.

</details>


### [111] [Private Embedding Lookup with Encrypted Compact Queries under Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03191)
*Daehyun Jang,Jaehee Kang,Hanee Rhee,Jung Hee Cheon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出独立向量评估（IVE）方法，将私密嵌入查找的向量生成成本从O(p log p)降至O(p)，通过使用加密索引的幂次构建线性无关向量，并借助预计算的正交离散余弦变换进行基变换，在FHE推理中实现高达78.4倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全同态加密（FHE）的隐私保护推理中，嵌入查找步骤需要将加密索引转换为选择向量，现有one-hot方法需要为每个坐标执行相等性测试，导致O(p log p)的同态操作成本过高，成为端到端加密推理的主要性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出独立向量评估（IVE）方法：放弃one-hot基向量，转而使用单个加密值的连续幂次构建线性无关向量（生成成本O(p)），然后通过预计算的正交离散余弦变换（DCT）实现基变换，恢复出正确的嵌入向量，同时利用DCT的正交性抑制误差放大。

Result: IVE将向量生成成本从O(p log p)降至O(p)，实现最高78.4倍的查询加速。在Enron-Spam数据集上的端到端加密FastText推理中，向量生成在加密推理时间中的占比从99.6%降至66.3%。

Conclusion: 私密嵌入查找仅需加密索引的线性无关表示而非one-hot基，IVE通过幂次向量+DCT基变换实现了更高效的FHE嵌入查找，显著提升了隐私保护推理的实际性能。

Abstract: Many NLP or recommendation models begin by mapping discrete client inputs to embedding vectors. Since inputs can reveal sensitive information, the embedding step must be protected in privacy-preserving inference. Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables inference over encrypted client data, but turns embedding lookup from simple table access into homomorphic computation. To keep the embedding table server-side and avoid transmitting encrypted embedding vectors from the client, we focus on server-side lookup: the client sends only a small encrypted index.
  Prior ICML 2024 work first builds a one-hot vector from the encrypted index before multiplying with the embedding table, and this one-hot generation is the dominant cost. One-hot-based methods are expensive in FHE: they construct a p-dimensional selection vector via an equality test for each coordinate, requiring $O(p \log p)$ total homomorphic operations.
  Our key observation is that private embedding lookup only requires a linearly independent representation of the encrypted index, not the one-hot basis itself. Building on it, we propose Independent Vector Evaluation (IVE). Instead of constructing a one-hot vector, IVE evaluates a linearly independent vector built from successive powers of a single encrypted value, reducing vector-generation cost to $O(p)$. It then recovers the same embedding vector via a precomputed change of basis, instantiated with an orthogonal Discrete Cosine Transform to mitigate error amplification.
  Our implementation shows IVE improves amortized lookup time by up to 78.4x over prior method. We further evaluate its impact on end-to-end encrypted FastText inference, where embedding lookup is a major cost in the shallow model. On Enron-Spam dataset, replacing one-hot generation with IVE reduces the share of vector generation in encrypted inference time from 99.6% to 66.3%.

</details>


### [112] [Generative AI-Enabled Refund Fraud in Chinese E-Commerce: Investigation on Merchants and Platform Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03215)
*Shuning Zhang,Eve He,Xiao Zhan,Shijing He,Robert Xiao,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 生成式AI（GenAI）使攻击者能够以极低成本伪造高度逼真的产品缺陷数字证据，颠覆了电商纠纷解决中“数字证据真实反映物理现实”的传统安全假设。通过对中国电商市场的17名商家和13名平台工作人员的半结构化访谈，本文揭示了GenAI驱动的可扩展伪造威胁，提出了涵盖交易、纠纷、物流和通信四个阶段的四类威胁向量分类法，并分析了平台和商家采用的AI辅助验证策略及其面临的实施障碍与根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商纠纷解决依赖数字证据真实反映物理现实的安全假设，而GenAI的发展使攻击者能够低成本伪造超逼真的产品缺陷证据，这一威胁模型的变化尚未被系统研究，亟需对GenAI赋能的电商欺诈新范式进行实证分析。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈方法，对中国电商市场的17名商家和13名平台工作人员进行深度访谈，基于访谈数据归纳出GenAI威胁的分类学框架。

Result: 提出了涵盖交易、纠纷、物流和通信四个阶段的四类GenAI威胁向量；发现平台和商家通过AI自动筛查和对抗性询问（如要求多角度视频）等手段提升攻击复杂度；同时识别出平台结构性约束和GenAI技术成熟度等阻碍防御措施落地的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 指出了隐私保护的跨平台欺诈数据库和嵌入可验证物质锚点的产品溯源机制等设计方向，为应对GenAI驱动的电商欺诈提供了系统性防御思路。

Abstract: E-commerce dispute resolution typically relies on the security assumption that digital evidence truthfully reflects physical reality. Generative AI (GenAI) invalidates this threat model, enabling attackers to fabricate hyper-realistic evidence of product defects at negligible cost. Through semi-structured interviews with merchants (N=17) and platform workers (N=13) in the Chinese e-commerce market, we characterize this shift toward GenAI-enabled scalable fabrication. We outline a taxonomy of four GenAI-enabled threat vectors across the transaction, dispute, logistics and communication phases, highlighting how attackers exploit GenAI to synthesize physically plausible product defects at scale. To mitigate these threats, platforms and merchants are adapting verification strategies, relying on AI tools for automated screening and adversarial interrogation (e.g., requesting multi-angle videos) to increase attack complexity. However, we find several challenges that hinder the adoption of these defenses, including implementation hurdles like structural platform constraints and fundamental limitations regarding the technical sophistication of GenAI. We conclude by outlining design implications for privacy-preserving cross-platform fraud databases, and traceability mechanisms such as embedding verifiable material anchors into the product.

</details>


### [113] [The Role of Domain-Specific Features in Malware Detection: A macOS Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03218)
*Biagio Montaruli,Andrea Oliveri,Savino Dambra,Davide Balzarotti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次利用macOS特有的域特定静态特征（如嵌入式证书、授权、持久化技术及关键系统API）训练机器学习恶意软件检测器，在41,129个样本数据集上达到98.50%的检测率，比现有方法平均提升16%；在9,000个新样本的时间泛化评估中保持99.50%的检测率，域特定特征贡献显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: macOS用户和企业系统日益普及，但恶意软件研究长期聚焦于Windows和Android，macOS恶意软件检测问题相对未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，利用macOS特有的域特定静态特征提升检测性能。

Method: 提取macOS二进制文件的域特定静态特征（如嵌入式证书、授权、持久化技术和关键系统API），结合机器学习模型训练分类器。在41,129样本（11,413良性+29,716恶意）上进行实验，并在9,000个新样本上评估时间泛化能力。

Result: 在41,129样本上达到98.50%检测率，平均比现有方法提升16%；时间泛化评估中达99.50%检测率，比现有最优方法提升50%，移除域特定特征后检测率下降15.92%。

Conclusion: 基于macOS域特定静态特征的机器学习检测器在检测率和时间泛化能力上均显著优于现有方法，域特定特征是提升检测性能的关键因素，数据集已向研究社区开放。
