Abstract: Despite the growing popularity of macOS among end users and enterprise systems, malware research has primarily focused on Windows and Android operating systems, leaving the problem of macOS malware detection relatively unexplored. Indeed, the specificity of the operating system and the unique characteristics of the Mach-O file format can play a fundamental role in the classification of unknown samples, drastically increasing the detection rate. In this work, for the first time in the literature, we employ new domain-specific features, i.e., static features specific to macOS binaries, such as embedded certificates, entitlements, persistence techniques and key system APIs, to train a machine learning malware detector. We perform a comprehensive experimental evaluation on a novel dataset of 41,129 samples, comprising 11,413 benign and 29,716 malicious executables, and demonstrate that our solution achieves state-of-the-art detection performance (98.50%), outperforming all existing approaches, with an average improvement of 16% in terms of detection rate. We also provide an in-depth analysis of the importance of the individual features, showing that our detector effectively leverages the new domain-specific features. Then, in order to evaluate the generalization capabilities of our detector over time, we perform a real-world evaluation on a new dataset of 9,000 fresh macOS executables. The results show that (i) our detector maintains a very high detection rate (99.50%), (ii) outperforms the state-of-the-art by 50%, and (iii) the domain-specific features are crucial for generalizing to novel malware samples, as their removal leads to a 15.92% drop in detection performance. Finally, we also release our dataset to the research community.

</details>


### [114] [Privilege Risk Evolution for Non-Human Identities: A Temporal Fiber Model for Cloud IAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03289)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出云权限治理中非人类身份(NHI)的等价关系包含结构等价(通过图纤维化捕捉快照权限配置)和时间等价(通过纤维转移图中的强连通分量捕捉循环权限状态)，时间等价类称为特权电路(privilege circuits)，并构建了三层分析框架，在大型Azure租户上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云权限治理隐式地将权限等价视为静态关系，忽略了非人类身份(NHI)权限随时间演化的动态特性，需要更精细的等价关系建模来理解权限的长期行为。

Method: 提出三层框架：(1)通过图纤维化构建权限图的空间商图；(2)组织稳定转移区间的谱系划分；(3)在谱系内进行窗口化SCC分析作为时间商图。通过大型Azure租户数据进行实证评估和回测验证。

Result: 在大型Azure租户上的实证评估支持了所提框架的有效性，回测表明早期观察到棘轮型特权电路可预测长期结构稳定性。

Conclusion: 非人类身份的权限等价包含不可约的结构和时间两个分量，特权电路是理解权限长期稳定性的关键概念，可用于预测云权限结构的演化趋势。

Abstract: Cloud permission governance implicitly treats permission equivalence as a static relation. We show that for non-human identities (NHIs), equivalence has two irreducible components: structural equivalence, capturing identical permission profiles at a snapshot via graph fibration, and temporal equivalence, capturing recurring permission states via strongly connected components (SCCs) in a fiber transition graph. We call the equivalence classes under temporal equivalence privilege circuits.
  We formalize a three-layer framework: (1) a spatial quotient of the permission graph via fibration, (2) a lineage partition organizing stable transition compartments, (3) windowed SCC analysis as a temporal quotient within lineages.
  Empirical evaluation on a large Azure tenant supports the framework. Backtesting demonstrates that early observation of ratchet-type privilege circuits predicts long-term structural stability.

</details>


### [115] [The Security Budget of Code LLMs: An Information-Theoretic Capacity-Security Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03308)
*Jianwei Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码LLM中功能容量（Cap）与扰动保留（Sec）之间的信息论权衡，证明了一个容量-安全预算不等式，并通过实验验证了该理论界，同时提出了一个与pass@1相关的上下文混合余弦信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手将自然语言提示作为软件开发接口，微小提示扰动会带来可用性和安全风险。需要从信息论角度理解代码LLM的功能能力与对扰动敏感度之间的根本权衡。

Method: 建立信息论框架，定义Cap=I(c*;c_π)和Sec=I(c_π;c̃_π)，证明Cap+Sec≤H(c*)+I(p;p̃)的预算不等式。通过确定性嵌入推论得到隐状态版本，利用tokenizer/gzip联合界给出模型无关的序列级任务熵上限。在多个模型、数据集和精度设置下进行实证验证，并提出上下文混合余弦作为生成-提示对齐信号。

Result: 六个验证行均满足嵌入检查(Cap+max_T Sec)/(H(z*)+max_T I(p;p̃))≤1，饱和度为0.27-0.92，定理松弛度为2.36-26.94 nats；三种子稳定性诊断平均饱和度0.87。上下文混合余弦与pass@1显著相关。自适应压力测试均保持正松弛度。

Conclusion: 理论界被实证验证有效，提示扰动保留存在信息论上限，上下文混合余弦可作为有用但独立的对齐信号。

Abstract: AI programming assistants make natural-language prompts a software-development interface, so small prompt perturbations become usability and security risks. We study an information-theoretic trade-off for code LLMs between functional capacity, $\Cap=\rmI(c^*;c_π)$, and perturbation retention, $\Sec=\rmI(c_π;\tilde c_π)$. Here $\Sec$ is a retention-channel quantity, not a direct measure of exploit success or vulnerable-code generation. For code completion modeled as $p\to c_π$ with perturbed prompt $\tilde p$, we prove $\Cap+\Sec\le \rmH(c^*)+\rmI(p;\tilde p)$, decomposing the budget into task entropy and prompt leakage. A deterministic-embedding corollary gives the hidden-state version, and a tokenizer/gzip companion bound gives a model-agnostic ceiling on sequence-level task entropy. Empirically, we estimate embedded $\Cap$ and $\Sec$ from output-only last-token hidden states, excluding prompt context from the $\Sec$ channel. Six individual validation rows across two models, two datasets, INT4/BF16 precision, and estimator ablations satisfy the embedded check $(\Cap+\max_T\Sec)/(\rmH(z^*)+\max_T\rmI(p;\tilde p))\le1$. Saturation is 0.27--0.92 and theorem slack is 2.36--26.94 nats; a separate three-seed stability diagnostic has mean saturation 0.87. A context-mixed cosine, used only as a per-problem generation-prompt alignment signal, correlates with pass@1 on CodeLlama-HumanEval ($ρ{=}0.36$, $p{<}10^{-4}$), Qwen-HumanEval ($ρ{=}0.22$, $p{=}0.005$), and CodeLlama-MBPP ($ρ{=}0.225$, $p{=}0.0038$; all $n{=}164$). Adaptive stress tests with a 23-perturbation pool, a fixed universal suffix, and prompt-embedding PGD all leave positive slack.

</details>


### [116] [dstack-capsule: Pod-Level Remote Attestation for Confidential Workloads on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03323)
*Yang Yang,Kevin Wang,Yuanhai Luo,Hang Yin,Jie Cai,Shunfan Zhou,Wenfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: dstack-capsule 是一个基于 Kubernetes 和 Intel TDX 的平台，通过允许多个 Pod 共享单个机密虚拟机，并利用双层认证架构（静态平台测量冻结在 RTMR[3] 中，动态 Pod 身份嵌入 TDX Quote 的 report_data 字段），实现了 Pod 级远程认证，避免了传统每 Pod 一个 VM 的高昂资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机密容器方案（如 CoCo）采用严格的"一个 Pod 一个 VM"模型，仅认证客户机操作系统栈，未验证容器级身份，且每个 VM 的资源开销过高，亟需一种更细粒度、低开销的 Pod 级远程认证方案。

Method: 提出双层认证架构：(1) Pod 级认证协议，将 Pod spec 摘要绑定到硬件签名的 TDX Quote；(2) 权限熔断机制，原子化地将节点从设置模式过渡到安全模式；(3) 多层沙箱，涵盖存储、运行时、准入、API 和网络隔离层；(4) 基于 Kubernetes 1.32、Intel TDX 和 Sysbox 的开源实现。

Result: 实现了 Pod 粒度的远程认证，同时消除了每 VM 隔离的资源开销，在安全性、认证正确性和性能方面均验证有效。

Conclusion: dstack-capsule 通过双层认证和权限熔断机制，在保持硬件级安全证明的同时显著降低了资源开销，为 LLM-as-a-Service 等机密云工作负载提供了一种高效的 Pod 级可信执行方案。

Abstract: The rise of LLM-as-a-Service and other confidential cloud workloads demands cryptographic proof that user data is processed in a trusted, untampered environment. Existing solutions, notably Confidential Containers (CoCo), enforce a strict "one Pod per VM" model that attests only the Guest OS stack, leaving container-level identity unverified and incurring prohibitive per-VM resource overhead. We present dstack-capsule, a Kubernetes platform that enables Pod-level remote attestation on Intel TDX by allowing multiple Pods to share a single Confidential VM while each retains independent, hardware-backed proof of identity. Our key insight is a two-layer attestation architecture: static platform measurements are frozen in RTMR[3] via an irreversible privilege fuse, while dynamic Pod identities (pod_uid, pod_spec_hash, workload_id) are embedded in the TDX Quote's report_data field and signed by hardware on every request. dstack-capsule introduces (1) a Pod-level attestation protocol binding Pod spec digests to hardware-signed Quotes; (2) a privilege fuse mechanism that atomically transitions a node from setup mode to secure mode; (3) a multi-layer sandbox spanning storage, runtime, admission, API, and network isolation layers; and (4) a complete open-source implementation based on Kubernetes 1.32, Intel TDX, and Sysbox. We evaluate the security properties, attestation correctness, and performance characteristics of dstack-capsule, demonstrating that it achieves Pod-granularity verification without the resource overhead of per-VM isolation.

</details>


### [117] [RogueMerge: Robust and Unified Attacks against LLM Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03344)
*Jinghuai Zhang,Yetian He,Kunlin Cai,Han Zhao,Fnu Suya,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RogueMerge框架，首次系统性地解决了生成式LLM在模型合并场景下面临的供应链攻击问题，通过联合优化、元学习式模拟和分布鲁棒优化，有效应对自回归解码退化、未知合并配置和异构攻击提示三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并通过聚合来自未验证公共平台的任务向量来组合专用能力，这暴露了关键的供应链攻击面：攻击者提供的恶意任务向量可直接写入模型权重，从而引发下游威胁。现有研究仅针对分类器的后门攻击且使用静态算术启发式方法，无法有效处理生成式LLM面临的三大挑战：(i)自回归解码中参数漂移逐token累积；(ii)攻击者对受害者合并配置无先验知识；(iii)静态向量无法泛化到优化时未见过的攻击提示。

Method: RogueMerge包含三个核心创新：(1)用联合优化替代静态算术，显式强制合并后攻击成功以应对自回归生成；(2)将攻击注入建模为随机min-max问题，通过元学习式模拟求解以应对未知合并设置；(3)采用分布鲁棒优化并推导出LLM规模下可处理的一阶泰勒近似（含可证明误差界），以跨异构攻击提示泛化。

Result: 在四种威胁、六种合并算法和超过170个合并LLM上，RogueMerge持续优于现有攻击方法，且在多种合并设置下保持稳定，并能抵抗标准防御手段。

Conclusion: RogueMerge是首个统一的原则性框架，系统解决了模型合并中对生成式LLM的供应链攻击挑战，为相关安全研究提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Model merging composes specialized capabilities into a single LLM by aggregating task vectors sourced from unverified public platforms, exposing a critical supply-chain attack surface: Because any malicious behavior can be encoded into a task vector, and merging grants third-party vectors direct write access to model weights, an attacker-provided task vector can enable or amplify diverse downstream threats. Prior work studies only backdoor attacks against model merging for classifiers using static arithmetic heuristics, which fail to effectively handle diverse attacks on generative LLMs for three reasons. (i) LLMs rely on autoregressive decoding, where the minor parameter drift introduced by merging compounds across tokens and rapidly degrades the attack. (ii) Attackers have no knowledge of the victim's merging configurations, causing a static attack vector optimized in isolation to be easily diluted or destroyed. (iii) Practical threat induction must generalize to attack prompts unseen during optimization, which static vectors cannot adequately encode. We present RogueMerge, the first principled, unified framework that addresses all three challenges. To handle autoregressive generation, we replace static arithmetic with a joint optimization that explicitly enforces attack success after merging. To handle unknown merging settings, we formulate attack injection as a stochastic min-max problem and solve it via meta-learning-style simulation. To generalize across heterogeneous attack prompts, we employ distributionally robust optimization and derive a tractable first-order Taylor approximation at LLM scale, with a provable error bound. Across four threats, six merging algorithms, and over 170 merged LLMs, RogueMerge consistently outperforms existing attacks. It also remains stable across diverse merging settings and resists standard defenses.

</details>


### [118] [ImageAuditor: Membership Inference Attack against Image-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03354)
*Jinghuai Zhang,Pengyue Yu,Zhexiao Lin,Kunlin Cai,Fnu Suya,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出首个面向图像检索增强生成（IRAG）的成员推理攻击方法ImageAuditor，通过奖励引导策略优化（RGPO）解决跨模态检索难题，并针对T2I和Q&A任务设计专用评分策略，在仅用4次查询的情况下实现超80%的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版权持有者需要审计不透明的网页爬取数据库中是否包含特定图像，而现有的文本RAG成员推理攻击因跨模态检索困难和判别信号提取问题无法直接迁移到IRAG场景。

Method: 将攻击查询分解为检索段和提取段：1) 检索段采用Reward-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO)从奖励排序的候选中更新随机策略，在跨模态嵌入空间中平衡探索与利用；2) 提取段通过分析MIA分数分布协同设计提示策略和评分规则，为T2I和Q&A任务分别实例化；3) 通过K-means聚类聚合多次查询信号进行最终成员判定。

Result: 在多种IRAG系统上，ImageAuditor仅用4次查询即可达到超过80%的AUROC，且在不同设置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ImageAuditor是首个针对IRAG的有效成员推理攻击方法，填补了跨模态RAG审计的空白，为版权合规提供了技术工具。

Abstract: Image-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (IRAG) conditions a frozen generator on reference images retrieved from an external database, supporting both text-to-image (T2I) and question answering (Q&A) tasks. Because these databases are opaque and web-scraped, copyright holders need ways to audit whether specific images appear in them. While prior work employs membership inference attacks (MIAs) to audit uni-modal, text-based RAG, they fail to transfer to IRAG due to two key challenges. First, cross-modal retrieval: text-RAG MIAs force retrieval of the target passage by injecting its content into the query, which is unavailable in IRAG since images cannot be embedded into text queries; even accurate image captions fail to bridge the modality gap. Second, discriminative signal extraction: text-RAG MIAs extract membership signals by prompting the generator to answer multiple questions over the target passage, whereas T2I generators in IRAG produce images rather than follow Q&A commands. To fill this gap, we introduce the first MIA tailored to IRAG, ImageAuditor, which decomposes each attack query into a retrieval segment and an extraction segment, enabling dedicated optimization for each challenge. For retrieval, we propose Reward-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO), which updates a stochastic policy from reward-ranked candidates to navigate the cross-modal embedding landscape and admits finite-sample optimality guarantees to balance exploration and exploitation. For extraction, we analyze the distribution of the MIA score to guide the co-design of the prompting strategy and scoring rule, and derive task-specific instantiations for T2I and Q&A tasks. We aggregate signals across queries via K-means clustering for reliable membership decisions. Across various IRAG systems, ImageAuditor exceeds 80% AUROC with only four queries per audited image and remains robust across diverse settings.

</details>


### [119] [AI Model Extraction Attacks: Bypassing Single-Client Assumptions in Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03381)
*Maxime Schwarzer,Johannes F. Loevenich,Gustavo Sánchez,Laurin Holz,Thies Möhlenhof,Tobias Hürten,Roberto Rigolin F. Lopes,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了模型提取攻击防御中“单客户端假设”（SCA）的根本缺陷，证明在高级持续性威胁（APT）等协同攻击者面前，现有防御机制（如PRADA）可通过简单的轮询查询分发策略绕过，甚至全局聚合方法也会被自适应流量混合瓦解，并提出开源框架CerberusAI用于可复现研究，呼吁向有状态、身份无关的防御架构范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御策略大多基于“单客户端假设”（SCA），即默认攻击来自孤立身份，但这一假设在高级持续性威胁（APT）等协同攻击者面前是否成立尚不明确，亟需系统性验证。

Method: 构建了模块化开源框架CerberusAI，模拟分布式攻击场景；通过轮询查询分发策略攻击PRADA等防御机制；采用自适应流量混合方法测试全局聚合防御的有效性。

Result: PRADA等经典防御机制可被基本轮询策略显著降低检测性能；全局聚合方法在自适应流量混合下变得操作上无效。

Conclusion: SCA在协同威胁下根本无效，需要向有状态、身份无关的防御架构进行范式转变。

Abstract: Ensuring the protection of Artificial Intelligence (AI) models deployed in military Command and Control (C2) systems and critical infrastructure is essential for maintaining information superiority. Model Extraction Attacks (MEAs) pose a significant threat, as they enable adversaries to replicate proprietary models, compromise protected information, and prepare offline adversarial attacks. However, current defense strategies predominantly rely on the Single Client Assumption (SCA), which is the implicit assumption that attacks originate from isolated identities. This work systematically demonstrates that the SCA is fundamentally invalid in the presence of coordinated threat actors, such as Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs). We introduce a modular, open-source framework called CerberusAI for reproducible model-stealing research, and use it to simulate distributed attack scenarios. Our empirical evaluation shows that well-established defense mechanisms, such as Protecting Against Deep Neural Network Model Stealing Attacks (PRADA), can be bypassed by basic round-robin query distribution strategies, resulting in a significant reduction in detection performance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that even global aggregation approaches can be rendered operationally useless through adaptive traffic mixing. These results highlight the need for a paradigm shift towards stateful, identity-independent defense architectures in the field of model extraction attacks. This paper was originally presented at the International Conference on Military Communication and Information Systems (ICMCIS), organized by the Information Systems Technology (IST) Scientific and Technical Committee, IST-224-RSY - the ICMCIS, held in Bath, United Kingdom, 12-13 May 2026 and won the best paper award.

</details>


### [120] [Operationalizing Cyber Attack Prediction: A Gap-Prioritized Framework with Dataset and Model Selection Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03386)
*Aminu Muhammad Auwal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了AI/机器学习在网络攻击预测中从研究到实际部署的五大障碍（数据集过时、攻击范围狭窄、可解释性、对抗鲁棒性、隐私伦理），提出优先级评估框架，并将45个基准数据集分为生产就绪、仅研究、不可用三类，为实际部署提供决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI和机器学习在网络攻击预测方面取得了进展，但理论研究与实际运维部署之间存在显著差距，亟需系统性地识别和优先解决阻碍落地的关键障碍。

Method: 对150+基准数据集和200+研究进行综合分析，提出差距优先级评估框架，从检测影响、实施成本和修复时间三个维度评估五大实现障碍；构建数据集质量评估框架，将45个基准数据集分为三类。

Result: 数据集过时和对抗鲁棒性被识别为最高优先级差距；模型可解释性是资源受限环境下最具成本效益的路径；45个基准数据集中部分被归为生产就绪、仅研究或不可用三类。

Conclusion: 本文通过提供实用的实施路线图和数据集质量评估框架，将学术研究成果转化为可操作的决策支持工具，以弥合研究与实践之间的鸿沟，推动面向生产环境的鲁棒AI驱动网络防御。

Abstract: While AI and machine learning for cyber attack prediction have advanced, a critical gap persists between theoretical research and practical operational deployment. Building on Ankalaki et al. (2025), this paper provides a comprehensive analysis of 150+ benchmark datasets and 200+ studies to identify and prioritize five implementation hurdles: (1) temporal dataset obsolescence, (2) narrow attack scope, (3) real-time model interpretability, (4) inadequate adversarial robustness, and (5) privacy/ethical concerns. We introduce a novel gap-prioritization framework that evaluates these limitations based on detection impact, implementation cost, and remediation time. Our analysis identifies dataset obsolescence and adversarial robustness as the highest-priority gaps, while highlighting model interpretability as the most cost-effective path for resource-constrained environments. To bridge the research-practice divide, we provide a practical implementation roadmap and a dataset quality assessment framework that classifies 45 benchmarks into production-ready, research-only, and unusable categories. This work translates academic findings into actionable decision-support tools for robust, production-oriented AI-driven cyber defense.

</details>


### [121] [Bastet: A Fine-Grained Expert-Labeled Dataset for DeFi Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03387)
*Wan-Hsuan Hsu,Wei-Hsin Wang,Cheng-Yu Liou,Ting-Rui Ke,Kentaroh Toyoda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Bastet是一个由专家标注的DeFi智能合约漏洞数据集，包含从394份Code4rena审计报告中收集的4,402个发现，其中849个由白帽安全研究人员通过双标注者共识流程标注，采用两层分类体系（46个标签和77个子标签），解决了现有数据集存在的Solidity版本过时、标注噪声大和标签粒度粗三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约漏洞检测评估数据集存在三个根本问题：基于过时Solidity版本（如v0.4），无法反映现代DeFi合约；依赖自动或LLM生成标注，引入幻觉导致的标签噪声；粗粒度的单层标签无法捕捉真实业务逻辑漏洞的语义复杂性。

Method: 从2021-2024年Code4rena竞赛审计报告中收集4,402个漏洞发现；由DeFiHackLabs社区的白帽安全研究人员通过双标注者共识工作流对849个发现进行人工标注；采用两层分类体系（46个标签和77个子标签）组织漏洞类型。

Result: 构建了Bastet数据集：4,402个漏洞发现（源自394份审计报告），其中849个经过完整人工标注，覆盖46个标签和77个子标签，所有标注均通过双标注者共识流程确保准确性。

Conclusion: Bastet通过真实审计案例、专家共识标注和细粒度两层分类体系，解决了现有数据集的关键缺陷，为LLM-based漏洞检测提供了更高质量、更符合现实的评估基准。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities in Decentralized Finance (DeFi) protocols resulted in over 1.49 billion USD in confirmed losses in 2024 alone, across 192 incidents [1]. As LLM-based vulnerability detection emerges as a promising approach to address these threats, the quality of evaluation datasets has become a critical bottleneck. Existing datasets suffer from three fundamental problems: they are built on outdated Solidity versions (e.g., v0.4) that no longer reflect modern DeFi contracts [5][6][7]; they rely on automated or LLM-generated annotations that introduce hallucination-driven label noise [9][10]; and they apply coarse single-layer labeling that fails to capture the semantic complexity of real-world business logic vulnerabilities [6][7][11][12]. We present Bastet, an expert-labeled DeFi smart contract vulnerability dataset that addresses all three problems through real-world audit findings (2021-2024), human expert annotation with discussion-based consensus, and a two-layer taxonomy of 46 Tags and 77 Subtags. Bastet comprises 4,402 findings collected from 394 Code4rena competitive audit reports spanning April 2021 to November 2024, of which 849 findings are fully annotated by white-hat security researchers from the DeFiHackLabs community. All annotations are produced through a two-annotator consensus workflow, ensuring label accuracy grounded in real-world vulnerability root causes.

</details>


### [122] [A Hybrid Approach For Malware Classification Using Secondary Features Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03432)
*Raja Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Mustaqeem,Haroon Elahi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过特征融合（API调用、固定与可变长度n-gram）和算法融合（基于投票的集成方法）来自动检测恶意软件并分类到其所属家族的方案，在微软数据集上达到AUC 0.989、准确率99.72%、log loss 0.01的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测方法无法将检测到的恶意软件分类到各自家族，阻碍了有效的恶意软件缓解；同时恶意软件数量（无论是变种还是新型）快速增长，使得检测与缓解成为复杂问题。

Method: 提取API调用、固定长度与可变长度n-gram等恶意软件特征，采用定制化特征选择方法进行特征融合；对预测模型采用基于投票的方法进行算法融合。

Result: 在微软提供的数据集上，二分类与多分类实验结果显示AUC为0.989，准确率为99.72%，log loss为0.01，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效且高效地自动检测恶意软件并将其分类到对应家族，对恶意软件缓解具有实际意义。

Abstract: The number of malware (either variant or novel) is rapidly increasing, making malware detection and mitigation a complex problem. One approach to improving malware mitigation is automatic detection and malware family classification. However, traditional malware detection methods cannot classify detected malware into their respective families, hindering effective malware mitigation. Consequently, this paper proposes a method to automate malware detection and classification of the detected malware into respective malware families. The proposed method uses feature fusion after extracting relevant malware features such as API calls and fixed and variable length n-grams with a customized feature selection method. Moreover, for the predictive model, a voting based approach is proposed for algorithm fusion. For the experimental evaluation of the proposed method, both binary and multi-class classification approaches are applied to the data set provided by Microsoft. Finally, the experimental results are compared with the state of the art. The experimental results indicate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach with an AUC of 0.989, accuracy of 99.72%, and a log loss of 0.01.

</details>


### [123] [Signals and Spoils: Speculative Oracle Extractable Value in the Era of Cross-Chain Interoperability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03434)
*Hasret Ozan Sevim,Christof Ferreira Torres*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Layer-2区块链中一种新型MEV——投机性预言机可提取价值（OEV），发现链上存在大量投机性清算行为（占清算者的57%及成功清算的39%），并证明了不同链间Chainlink预言机更新存在时间差，可被利用进行跨链OEV提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Layer-2区块链缺乏公开mempool，导致MEV提取策略变为概率性，产生大量交易垃圾，浪费区块空间并推高费用。作者旨在量化这一现象并探索跨链OEV的可行性与规律。

Method: 提出检测投机性清算的方法论，在Arbitrum、Base、Optimism三个链上进行实证分析；系统分析Chainlink去中心化预言机网络在不同链上的配置参数（偏差阈值、心跳间隔、提交价格观察），构建包含63个Chainlink数据源、12,009次价格更新及超过100,000条预言机观测的数据集，关联2,986次Aave清算事件。

Result: 2025年10月10日数据显示，三个链上64个投机性清算者占Aave所有清算者的57%，831次成功投机清算占所有成功清算的39%。跨链分析表明，独立DON近乎同时消费相同链下价格数据但发布时间不同，Optimism上的Chainlink更新可预测随后Arbitrum和Base上的更新，形成统计学上可预测的跨链利用窗口。

Conclusion: Layer-2区块链存在显著的投机性MEV问题，且跨链预言机更新延迟差异可被系统性地利用进行跨链OEV提取，这对Layer-2生态的安全性和效率构成挑战。

Abstract: A new form of Maximal Extractable Value (MEV), termed speculative MEV, has emerged across Layer-2 blockchains. Unlike Ethereum mainnet, many Layer-2 systems lack a public mempool, forcing extraction strategies to become probabilistic: searchers emit multiple identical transactions hoping to capture an opportunity first. This generates substantial transaction spam, increasing fees and wasting block space. We investigate speculative Oracle Extractable Value (OEV), a form of MEV associated with liquidating undercollateralized loans via speculative backrunning of oracle price updates. We propose a methodology for detecting speculative liquidations in the wild and apply it across Arbitrum, Base, and Optimism. On October 10, 2025, we identify 64 speculative liquidators on Aave (57% of all detected liquidators) and 831 successful speculative liquidations (39% of all successful liquidations across the three chains). We further examine whether latency differences in oracle price feed updates across blockchains can be exploited for cross-chain OEV. Specifically, we ask whether a searcher can observe oracle updates on one chain and frontrun liquidation opportunities on another. We systematically analyze Chainlink Decentralized Oracle Network (DON) configurations (deviation thresholds, heartbeat intervals, and submitted price observations) across Arbitrum, Base, Ethereum, and Optimism. Our dataset comprises 63 Chainlink feeds, 12,009 price updates, and over 100,000 oracle observations linked to 2,986 Aave liquidations. We show that independent DONs consume largely identical off-chain price data nearly simultaneously yet publish updates at different times, creating statistically predictable cross-chain exploitation windows. We demonstrate that Chainlink updates on Optimism can predict subsequent updates on Arbitrum and Base, enabling speculative cross-chain OEV extraction.

</details>


### [124] [FORGE: Multi-Agent Graduated Exploitation and Detection Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03453)
*Farooq Shaikh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FORGE 是一个多智能体系统，通过渐进式利用深度（L0-L3四级）桥接漏洞利用生成、优先级排序和检测规则工程三个孤立的社区。在603个CVE上实现67.8%的端到端L1+利用，每CVE成本1.50美元，并生成高保真度的Sigma和Snort检测规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞披露数量远超组织评估能力，但三个相关研究社区（PoC生成、漏洞优先级排序、检测规则工程）彼此孤立运行。现有自动化利用生成系统仅报告二元通过/失败结果，丢弃了部分进度信息，无法为其他两个社区提供有用信号。

Method: FORGE 包含五个专门智能体（Intel、Generator、Planner、Exploit、Detector），以固定流水线执行：(1) 从CVE元数据生成目标脆弱应用，(2) 通过LLM主导向的多轮交互利用评估，基于四层级分类法（L0无证据到L3完全攻陷），(3) 基于OpenTelemetry利用追踪生成Sigma和Snort检测规则。采用渐进式利用深度作为桥梁机制，并构建分层知识架构以跨评估积累经验。

Result: 在CVE-GENIE数据集的603个CVE上评估，实现67.8%的端到端L1+利用成功率，每CVE成本1.50美元，覆盖8种编程语言和187个CWE类型。EPSS或CVSS评分段对利用成功率影响不大（均接近68%）。L2+利用生成的检测规则比L1规则具有显著更高的span-normalized grounding（p=0.035），93.4%的Snort规则在合成良性语料上产生零误报。

Conclusion: FORGE 成功通过渐进式利用深度桥接了三个孤立的研究社区，证明了模式级可达性与基于元数据的优先级排序是正交的，且更深层次的利用能为检测工程提供更丰富的行为轨迹，从而生成更高质量的检测规则。

Abstract: Vulnerability disclosure volumes now far exceed organizational assessment capacity, yet three adjacent research communities (proof-of-concept generation, vulnerability prioritization, and detection rule engineering) operate largely in isolation. Existing automated exploit generation systems report binary pass/fail outcomes, discarding partial progress and producing no signal for the other two communities. This paper presents FORGE, a multi-agent system that bridges these three silos through graduated exploitation depth. Five specialized agents (Intel, Generator, Planner, Exploit, and Detector) execute in a fixed pipeline that (1) generates targeted vulnerable applications from CVE metadata, (2) conducts coached, multi-turn exploitation assessed by an LLM-primary oracle on a four-level taxonomy (L0: no evidence through L3: full compromise), and (3) produces Sigma and Snort detection rules grounded in OpenTelemetry exploitation traces. Graduated depth is the bridging mechanism: deeper exploitation yields richer behavioral traces for detection engineering, while depth data across scoring bands provides ground truth for prioritization validation. A tiered knowledge architecture accumulates intelligence across assessments, transferring build and exploitation experience to subsequent CVEs. Evaluation on 603 CVEs from the CVE-GENIE dataset achieves 67.8% end-to-end L1+ exploitation at USD 1.50 per CVE across eight languages and 187 CWE types. Exploitation rates remain near 68% regardless of EPSS or CVSS band, indicating that pattern-level reachability is orthogonal to metadata-based prioritization. Detection rules from L2+ exploitation achieve significantly higher span-normalized grounding than L1-derived rules (p=0.035), and 93.4% of generated Snort rules produce zero false positives against a synthetic benign corpus.

</details>


### [125] [NeuroArmor: Safe-Variant-Guided Representation Consistency for Selective Re-Anchoring in Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03486)
*Zhongyang Lin,Ziran Zhao,Feifei Zhai,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NeuroArmor是一种白盒运行时防御方法，通过为每个提示构建安全变体作为局部安全参考，在隐藏状态空间中进行一致性检查，并将异常提示路由到拒绝分支或恢复分支，从而在保持低误报率的同时显著降低越狱攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法对所有提示采用相同策略，难以在安全性和有用性之间取得平衡，要么过度拦截良性敏感请求，要么无法有效防御多样化越狱攻击。

Method: NeuroArmor为每个输入提示构建K个安全变体作为局部安全参考；在隐藏状态空间中将原始提示状态与安全参考进行比较；检测到异常时，恶意提示路由到拒绝分支，边界良性提示路由到有帮助的恢复分支。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上，恶意攻击成功率从41.56%降至1.57%，良性误报率从30.26%降至22.05%；外部评判和人工行为评估表明未拦截输出操作性危害更低。

Conclusion: NeuroArmor通过提示特定的一致性检查、路由和选择性干预，提供了一种更有效的运行时越狱防御策略。

Abstract: Large language models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that hide harmful intent behind seemingly ordinary requests such as role-play, translation, encoding, adversarial suffixes, and multi-turn buildup. Existing defenses still struggle to handle these attacks without over-blocking benign but sensitive requests, partly because they often apply the same action to every prompt and therefore fail to balance safety and helpfulness. We propose NeuroArmor, a white-box runtime defense that uses prompt-specific safe variants as a local safety reference for deciding when intervention is needed and, once triggered, as safe targets for intervention. For each prompt, NeuroArmor builds K safe variants, compares the prompt state against this local safe reference in hidden-state space, and routes anomalies either to a refusal branch for malicious prompts or to a helpful recovery branch for borderline benign prompts. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, NeuroArmor reduces malicious attack success rate (ASR) from 41.56% to 1.57% while lowering benign false positive rate (FPR) on the shared benign pool from 30.26% to 22.05%; matched baselines remain substantially weaker on this trade-off. External-judge and manual behavioral evaluations further show that the remaining non-blocked outputs are much less likely to be operationally harmful. Overall, NeuroArmor provides a more effective runtime strategy for jailbreak defense by combining prompt-specific consistency checking, routing, and selective intervention.

</details>


### [126] [Privacy-Preserving High-Resolution Image Gradient Computation Based on Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03513)
*Yufei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大图像的基于多密文同态加密的隐私保护框架，通过分块处理、并行加密、重复打包技术及新的引导放置策略，高效实现高分辨率图像的加密与Sobel算子计算，并基于符号函数提出了新的倒数函数多项式近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同态加密图像处理研究多聚焦于低分辨率图像，高分辨率图像因参数扩大导致计算开销剧增，缺乏有效的隐私保护处理方法。

Method: 将大图像分割为多个子图像以维持小HE参数并减小密钥规模；采用并行处理子图像密文及新的引导放置策略降低加密开销；通过重复打包技术优化大图像卷积运算；基于符号函数提出新的多项式近似方法实现倒数函数计算，以改进Sobel算子梯度方向计算。

Result: 在保持较小HE参数和密钥尺寸的前提下，显著降低了加密开销，实现了高效的密文域大图像Sobel算子计算。

Conclusion: 所提多密文框架有效解决了高分辨率图像在隐私保护场景下的计算效率问题，所提出的倒数函数多项式近似方法具有通用性，可推广至其他基于HE的协议。

Abstract: With growing emphasis on privacy protection, homomorphic encryption (HE) has emerged as a core method for privacy-preserving image processing, as it enables operations directly on encrypted data. However, existing research predominantly focuses on low-resolution image processing, and techniques for privacy-preserving high-resolution image processing remain underexplored. As the image size increases, the HE parameters must be adjusted accordingly, and directly applying existing methods can lead to significant computational overhead. In this work, we propose a multi-ciphertext privacy-preserving framework for large images, enabling efficient image encryption and computation under the semi-honest model. Specifically, we divide the large image into multiple sub-images, which allows us to maintain smaller HE parameters and reduce key size. By parallel processing the sub-image ciphertexts and introducing a new bootstrapping placement strategy, we significantly reduce encryption overhead and enhance user experience. On the server side, we optimize the large image convolution operation through a repeated packing technique and implement the Sobel operator computation based on HE. To improve gradient direction calculation for the Sobel operator, we introduce a new polynomial approximation method for the reciprocal function based on the sign function, which can be applied to other HE-based protocols.

</details>


### [127] [High-Precision APT Malware Attribution with Out-of-Scope Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03523)
*Peter Williams,Adam Sobey,Erisa Karafili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于排序二分类器并带有显式弃权机制的高精度APT恶意软件归因方法，解决开放环境下未知APT组样本的误归因问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APT恶意软件归因方法通常作为闭集分类器在有限已知APT组上训练和评估，但实际运营环境中会遇到训练中未出现的APT组样本，闭集分类器会错误地将这些样本分配到已知组，产生误导性归因。

Method: 不训练单一多分类器，而是为每个APT组训练并调优两个二分类器，按验证性能排序后顺序应用。仅当分类器提供充分证据时才进行归因，否则弃权。

Result: 在APT Malware数据集上精度高于先前公布结果。在最具挑战性的设置中（87%测试样本来自60个未参与训练的APT组），该方法对94%的域外样本弃权，同时在被分类的样本上保持92%精度和95%选择性准确率。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理开放环境中的未知APT组样本，通过弃权机制避免误归因，同时保持高精度。

Abstract: Early attribution of Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) activity can help defenders prioritise investigation, select countermeasures, and reduce the impact of an intrusion. Malware provides useful attribution evidence, but automated APT malware attribution remains difficult in practice. Existing approaches are typically trained and evaluated as closed-set classifiers over a limited number of known APT groups. In operational environments, however, classifiers are likely to encounter samples from groups not represented during training. Closed-set classifiers are then forced to assign such samples to known groups, producing unsupported and potentially misleading attributions. We present a high-precision APT malware attribution method based on ranked binary classifiers with explicit abstention. Rather than training a single multi-class classifier, our approach trains and tunes two binary classifiers per APT group, ranks the classifiers by validation performance, and applies them sequentially. A sample is attributed only when a classifier provides sufficient evidence; otherwise, it abstains. We evaluate the method on the APT Malware dataset and on a larger combined dataset designed to stress-test out-of-scope behaviour. On the APT Malware dataset, the method achieves higher precision than previously published results on the same dataset. In the most challenging setting, where 87% of test samples came from 60 APT groups excluded from training, the method abstained on 94% of out-of-scope samples while maintaining 92% precision and 95% selective accuracy on the samples it classified.

</details>


### [128] [Towards Intrusion Detection Systems for RPL-based IoT Networks using Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03530)
*Elias Lunderbye,Sourasekhar Banerjee,Christian Rohner,Andreas Johnsson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探索了基础模型（MOMENT）在RPL物联网网络中检测和识别多种攻击（Blackhole、DIS flooding、Worst Parent、Local Repair）的能力，通过微调实现多类攻击识别，在Cooja仿真数据集上取得了与现有方法相当的检测性能和优异的攻击类型区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的入侵检测系统在物联网攻击检测上虽有前景，但现有方法在区分多种攻击类型方面仍有局限，本文旨在探索基础模型在此任务上的潜力。

Method: 微调MOMENT基础模型进行多类攻击识别，基于Cooja仿真环境收集的RPL相关统计数据集（包含正常操作与四种攻击场景）。

Result: 该方法达到了与现有最优方法相当的攻击检测性能，并在区分不同攻击类型方面表现出色。

Conclusion: 基础模型在RPL物联网网络攻击检测与识别中具有潜力，初始结果令人鼓舞。

Abstract: AI-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) have shown promise in detecting attacks on IoT systems. In this work, we explore the use of foundation models to detect and identify attacks, with a specific focus on RPL-based IoT networks. We study multiple attack types, attack variations, and network configurations, and provide insights into the performance of foundation models for attack identification. Specifically, we fine-tune the MOMENT foundation model for multi-class attack identification. Our evaluation is based on a dataset containing RPL-related statistics collected under normal operation and under Blackhole, DIS flooding, Worst Parent, and Local Repair attacks, generated in a Cooja simulation environment. The initial results are promising. The approach achieves attack-detection performance comparable to state-of-the-art methods, while also demonstrating strong performance in distinguishing between different attack types.

</details>


### [129] [Channel Chart Location Privacy Based on Geo-Indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03571)
*Atsu Kokuvi Angélo Passah,Rodrigo C. de Lamare,Arsenia Chorti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向信道制图的几何感知隐私保护机制（MNPL），通过在潜在流形中注入符合局部结构的噪声，实现了图表位置不可区分性（CLI），在提供强隐私保障的同时保持了信道制图用于基于位置服务的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道制图虽因使用伪位置而具有固有隐私优势，但缺乏形式化的隐私保证。本文旨在为信道制图提供正式的位置隐私保护框架。

Method: 提出图表位置不可区分性（CLI）框架，将地理不可区分性（GI）扩展到信道制图表示；基于标准平面拉普拉斯机制，设计了几何感知的马氏范数平面拉普拉斯（MNPL）机制，通过注入与图表局部结构对齐的噪声来扰动信道制图。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的MNPL机制在提供强隐私保证的同时，有效保持了信道制图在基于位置服务任务中的可用性。

Conclusion: 本文为信道制图建立了正式的位置隐私框架，所提出的几何感知隐私机制能够在隐私保护和图表可用性之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Channel charting enables location-based services (LBSs) without requiring explicit position information by using pseudo-locations from the channel chart. While this property implies inherent privacy advantages, it does not provide formal privacy guarantees. In this work, we address location privacy in channel charting referred to as chart location indistinguishability (CLI), which extends geo-indistinguishability (GI) to channel charting representations. In order to achieve CLI, a standard planar Laplace mechanism is investigated and a geometry-aware Mahalanobis norm planar Laplace (MNPL) mechanism is devised. The proposed MNPL mechanism perturbs the channel chart by injecting noise aligned with the local structure of the chart. In the CLI framework with MNPL, privacy is defined in latent channel chart manifolds using locally adaptive covariance derived from chart neighborhoods, while preserving manifold topology under privacy constraints. In addition, differential privacy is considered as a privacy baseline. The proposed approach is evaluated across multiple channel charting schemes. The performance is assessed using utility metrics such as quality loss (QL) and range query error (RQE), as well as geometry-aware metrics including trustworthiness (TW) and continuity (CT). Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed privacy mechanism provides strong privacy guarantees while preserving the channel chart for LBSs tasks.

</details>


### [130] [Testing LLM Arithmetic Reasoning Generalization with Automatic Numeric-Remapping Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03606)
*Malia Barker,Bishal Lakha,Edoardo Serra,Francesco Gullo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化的数值重映射攻击算法，用于测试大语言模型在算术推理中的脆弱性。实验发现，即使保持推理程序不变，GSM8K数据集上的模型条件准确率仍下降12-25个百分点，而MAWPS和MultiArith则保持高鲁棒性，说明数值鲁棒性高度依赖数据集结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明大语言模型对数值变化敏感，但缺乏在严格条件下（保留原始推理程序、避免大数压力测试）系统评估这种脆弱性的工作。作者希望探究当仅对数值进行小幅、保持模式的更改时，模型是否仍然表现不稳定。

Method: 提出一种自动化的数值重映射攻击算法：首先从算术问题中推导出问题特定的符号表示，生成受约束的数值重映射，重新计算标准答案，然后通过LLM生成的编辑计划执行确定性编辑以实现问题转换。采用分阶段验证和高置信度审计来保留可靠的攻击样本，使流水线在有限人工干预下可扩展。

Result: 在GSM8K上，条件准确率下降12.16到25.82个百分点；在MAWPS和MultiArith上，大多数攻击后的准确率接近或超过98%。数值重映射鲁棒性高度依赖于数据集结构。

Conclusion: 即使推理程序被保留且答案被重新计算，GSM8K仍然对数值变化敏感，而更短、更规则的数据集则更为鲁棒。这表明数值鲁棒性不是一个通用能力，而是与数据集特性密切相关。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong performance on arithmetic reasoning benchmarks, and one common response to arithmetic brittleness is to delegate computation to code. Yet models are still often used in settings where they must reason directly from natural language, and trustworthy models should solve small-number arithmetic word problems without external tools. Prior work shows that LLMs are sensitive to numerical variation: a model may solve an original problem but fail on structurally similar variants requiring the same reasoning procedure with different numbers. We ask whether this fragility persists under a stricter setting involving small, schema-preserving numeric changes that retain the original reasoning program and avoid large-number stress tests. We introduce an automatic algorithm for generating numeric-remapping attacks on arithmetic word problems. Unlike template-based perturbation methods requiring manual schemas or constraints, our approach derives problem-specific symbolic representations, generates constrained numeric remappings, recomputes gold answers, and realizes transformed questions through deterministic edits guided by LLM-generated edit plans. Stage-wise validation and a high-confidence audit retain reliable attacks, making the pipeline scalable with limited human intervention. We evaluate DeepSeek-R1 (70B), Gemma4 (31B), and GPT-OSS (120B) on GSM8K, MAWPS, and MultiArith. On GSM8K, completed runs show conditional accuracy drops of 12.16 to 25.82 percentage points. MAWPS and MultiArith are far more stable, with most attacked accuracies near or above 98%. These results show that numeric-remapping robustness depends strongly on dataset structure: GSM8K remains sensitive even when reasoning programs are preserved and answers are recomputed, while shorter, more regular datasets are more robust.

</details>


### [131] [Q-FE: A Quantum-Native 6G Far-Edge Architecture Securing Industrial IoT Digital Twins via CSIDH-PQC and Asynchronous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03611)
*Vincenzo Sammartino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了Q-FE，一种面向6G超密集工业物联网的量子原生边缘架构，集成微数字孪生(μDT)、跨层后量子密钥交换(CSIDH-512)和异步联邦学习(AFL)三大组件，在亚毫秒时延、10⁻⁷级可靠性和抗量子安全方面取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6G架构将数字孪生计算集中于云端或MEC服务器，导致往返时延过高；同时依赖经典公钥密码学，在"先收密后解密"威胁模型下易受量子攻击，无法满足超密集IIoT场景中亚毫秒时延、超高可靠性和长期密码安全的需求。

Method: 提出Q-FE架构，包含三个协同设计的组件：(i) 微数字孪生(μDT)与6G基站和高能力端点共置；(ii) 跨层后量子密钥交换模块，将CSIDH-512同源密钥材料嵌入MAC层控制帧(密钥≤64字节，避免包分片)；(iii) 基于轻量级DAG智能合约的异步联邦学习协议，消除掉队者瓶颈并防御模型投毒和女巫攻击。

Result: 端到端仿真(NS-3+PySyft)表明：Q-FE将MAC层开销比ML-KEM/Kyber-1024降低62%；P99.9 URLLC时延维持在0.78毫秒；全局模型收敛速度比同步联邦学习提升31%；协议复杂度为O(N log R)每聚合轮；μDT切换迁移在10⁴次模拟事件中完成时间为1.9±0.3毫秒。

Conclusion: Q-FE架构在超密集IIoT场景中同时满足了亚毫秒级URLLC时延、抗量子安全性和高效联邦学习的要求，形式化威胁模型验证了其对量子窃听、模型投毒和女巫攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks will underpin ultra-dense Industrial IoT (IIoT) ecosystems in which resource-constrained Far-Edge devices -- autonomous mobile robots, industrial actuators, connected vehicles -- must simultaneously satisfy sub-millisecond latency, $10^{-7}$-class reliability, and decades-long cryptographic security. Current architectures delegate Digital Twin (DT) computation to centralised cloud or Mobile Edge Computing (MEC) servers, incurring prohibitive round-trip latency, and rely on classical public-key cryptography vulnerable to quantum attacks under the harvest-now, decrypt-later (HNDL) threat model. We propose Q-FE, a Quantum-Native 6G Far-Edge architecture integrating three co-designed components: (i) Micro-Digital Twins ($μ$DTs) co-located with 6G base stations and high-capability endpoints; (ii) a Cross-Layer Post-Quantum Key Exchange module embedding CSIDH-512 isogeny key material directly within MAC-layer control frames, exploiting the scheme's uniquely compact keys ($\le 64$ bytes) to avoid packet fragmentation; and (iii) an Asynchronous Federated Learning (AFL) protocol governed by lightweight DAG smart contracts at MEC nodes, eliminating straggler bottlenecks and preventing model-poisoning and Sybil attacks without exposing raw data. End-to-end simulations (NS-3 + PySyft) demonstrate that Q-FE reduces MAC-layer overhead by 62% versus ML-KEM/Kyber-1024, maintains P99.9 URLLC latency at 0.78 ms, and accelerates global-model convergence by 31% over synchronous Federated Learning. Protocol complexity analysis confirms $O(N \log R)$ per aggregation round, and $μ$DT handover migration completes in $1.9 \pm 0.3$ ms across $10^4$ simulated events. A formal threat model confirms resilience against quantum eavesdropping, model-poisoning, and Sybil attacks.

</details>


### [132] [Ghost: Plausible Yet Unlearnable Trajectories via On-Manifold Substitution for Next-POI Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03711)
*Zhenyu Yu,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Ghost框架，通过流形对齐的轨迹扰动生成不可学习序列，防范基于签到轨迹的未来位置预测攻击，在保持地理语义合理性的同时抵御多种净化攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发布签到轨迹会泄露用户未来位置的强预测器，现有图像域不可学习示例方法无法直接迁移到轨迹域，因为需要保持数据的地理语义合理性并抵御利用随机防御结构的净化攻击。

Method: 提出Ghost框架，利用冻结的轨迹语言模型将每次替换扰动导向真实轨迹流形，使去噪桥接对手无法逆转，上下文无关频率表对手恢复近均匀分布。

Result: 在两个基准数据集和四种攻击姿态下，Ghost的保护差距与最强确定性基线PGD相当，在双字母自适应净化对手下达到最低恢复精度，保护-净化抵抗平面性能在PGD的一个单元格标准差内。

Conclusion: 流形先验取代了先前随机防御的熵下限控制，即使20%的配对泄露，频率表对手的生存差距仍保持在0.04以内，验证了方法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: A publisher who releases check-in trajectories inadvertently publishes a strong predictor of every user's future locations. We address this risk by generating unlearnable trajectories, perturbed sequences that yield victim models with degraded next-Point-of-Interest (next-POI) accuracy on clean test inputs. Direct ports of image-domain unlearnable examples fail on two counts. The published data must remain geographically and semantically plausible, and the perturbation must resist purification adversaries that exploit the structure of randomized defences. We propose Ghost, a manifold-aligned framework whose perturbations look like plausible human check-in sequences yet leave no learnable signal behind. Ghost steers each substitution onto the real-trajectory manifold through a frozen trajectory language model, so a denoising-bridge adversary has nothing to invert and a context-free frequency-table adversary recovers a near-uniform distribution. Across two standard benchmarks, and four attacker postures, Ghost achieves protection-gap competitive with the strongest deterministic baseline (PGD) while attaining the lowest restored accuracy under the bigram adaptive purification adversary on both datasets, and lies within one per-cell standard deviation of PGD on the protection-versus-purification-resistance plane. Ablations confirm the manifold prior subsumes the entropy-floor knob of prior randomized defences, with the frequency-table adversary's survival gap remaining within 0.04 even when twenty percent of the pairs are leaked.

</details>


### [133] [Don't Trust Us: A privacy-by-design android malware detection pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03714)
*Emmanuele Massidda,Diego Soi,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种隐私内嵌的Android恶意软件检测方法，在静态分析阶段使用Drebin特征+SVM+双拒绝阈值规则，仅在低置信度时将样本移交至沙箱动态分析，避免访问任何用户敏感数据，在2024-2025时间分割数据集上F1达0.87，仅6.7%样本需二次分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Android恶意软件检测方法依赖收集和处理用户敏感数据，隐私保护通常事后进行（如匿名化、加密、联邦学习），仍要求用户信任拥有设备特权访问权限的系统。作者认为应从根本上消除而非管理这种信任需求。

Method: 1. 形式化定义了一组隐私内建设计需求；2. 静态分析阶段：按Drebin表示提取APK特征并向量化，输入SVM分类器；3. 双拒绝阈值规则：高置信度直接输出决策，低置信度样本移交至沙箱动态分析；4. 动态分析在沙箱隔离环境中进行，用户真实信息永不进入分析循环。

Result: 在2024-2025时间分割数据集上，静态分析阶段F1达0.87，仅6.7%测试样本需移交动态分析；沙箱动态分析能高置信度识别恶意行为，且不提取任何敏感数据。

Conclusion: 强检测性能可在不牺牲用户隐私的前提下实现，证明了隐私内建式Android恶意软件检测的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Android malware detection increasingly relies on collecting and processing sensitive user data, including device identifiers, network artifacts, and runtime traces, while privacy is too often treated as a secondary concern. Existing privacy-aware approaches typically enforce privacy after data collection, for example, through anonymization, encryption, or federated learning, yet still require access to user information and therefore demand a high level of user trust in systems that already operate with privileged access to device activity. We argue that this requirement should be removed rather than managed. Android malware detection should be privacy-aware by design, so that effective analysis does not depend on sensitive data being accessed in the first place. To this end, we first formalize a set of design requirements for privacy-by-design detection and then implement each requirement in a comprehensive pipeline. First, static analysis is performed to extract relevant data from each APK, following the Drebin representation, which is then submitted to an SVM after vectorization. The model is equipped with a dual-reject threshold rule that either commits to a confident decision or defers uncertain samples to a dynamic analysis stage within a sandboxed environment, so that genuine user information never enters the analysis loop. Results confirm that, on a temporally split dataset spanning from 2024 to 2025, the pipeline achieves an F1 score of 0.87 with the first static analysis stage, deferring only 6.7% of test samples to secondary dynamic analysis. Additionally, dynamic sandboxing helps recognize applications' maliciousness with high confidence without extracting any sensitive data. These results demonstrate that strong detection performance is achievable without sacrificing user privacy.

</details>


### [134] [Same Weights, Different Robot: A Deployment Safety View of VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03724)
*Jianwei Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VLA策略的部署存在安全鸿沟：相同检查点因动作反归一化和控制器约定的差异可能产生不同物理动作。本文形式化定义了可执行策略规范问题，发现元数据键值替换可导致LIBERO基准测试中成功率从100%降至0%，证明动作空间元数据应作为可执行策略的一部分在部署前检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略被当作检查点定义对象，忽略了相同归一化输出在反归一化和控制器约定下会变成不同物理动作的问题，造成部署安全鸿沟：安全审查可认证检查点，但无法保证最终执行的机器人策略安全。

Method: 形式化定义可执行策略规范问题，将VLA策略分解为学习模型、动作表示、元数据选择的反归一化器和控制器约定四个组成部分；针对分位数式动作归一化，推导出闭式元数据错配变换和ExecSpec证书，无需模型推理或实际 rollout 即可衡量动作空间语义漂移。

Result: 在LIBERO-Goal回放中，替换近似兄弟元数据键值导致非夹爪6个动作维度平均漂移0.199，成功率从28/28降至2/28；LIBERO-Spatial成功率从26/26降至0/26；全部四项Object替换成功率为0/28；Long任务成功率为0/23或1/23。通过身份键、回放有效性、无操作过滤等多种对照实验排除了其他解释。

Conclusion: 动作空间元数据是可执行策略的组成部分，应在部署前进行检查。本结果不涉及闭环或硬件安全性，但支持更狭义的部署安全视角。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are often treated as checkpoint-defined objects: if the weights, prompt, and benchmark suite match, the deployment is assumed to be the same policy. Robot execution breaks this assumption because the same normalized model output can become a different physical action after action unnormalization and controller conventions are applied. This creates a deployment-safety gap: safety review can certify the checkpoint while missing the executable robot policy that reaches the controller. We formalize this gap as an executable policy specification problem: a VLA policy includes the learned model, action representation, metadata-selected unnormalizer, and controller-facing conventions. Under this view, identical checkpoints can be executable-inequivalent. For quantile-style action normalization, we derive a closed-form metadata mismatch transform and an ExecSpec certificate that measures action-space semantic drift without model inference or rollout. On LIBERO-Goal replay, substituting a plausible sibling metadata key yields mean drift 0.199 over six non-gripper action dimensions and reduces success from 28/28 to 2/28 under full substitution. On LIBERO-Spatial replay, the same substituted key reduces success from 26/26 to 0/26. The same full-substitution protocol gives 0/28 success for all four Object substitutions and 0/23 or 1/23 success on Long. Identity-key, replay-validity, no-op filtering, raw-vs-correct replay, mask/gripper, synthetic upper-bound, and OpenVLA-style unnormalizer interface checks rule out several simpler explanations. These results do not certify closed-loop or hardware safety. They support a narrower deployment-safety view: action-space metadata is part of the executable policy and should be checked before rollout.

</details>


### [135] [$π$Creds: Privately Inferred Credentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03771)
*Samuel Breckenridge,Dani Vilardell,Derek Leung,Andrés Fábrega,James Austgen,Farinaz Koushanfar,Ari Juels*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出πCreds，一种利用可信LLM推理从未经结构化认证数据中生成隐私保护的、兼容遗留系统的去中心化可验证凭证系统，并形式化了两种新威胁模型：SCAE（源受限对抗样本）和ACPP（认证隐蔽谓词投毒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化可验证凭证系统基于零知识证明，复杂且局限于结构化数据的谓词验证，实际部署有限。LLM的语义推理能力可大幅扩展可认证声明的范围，但引入新的应用层威胁需形式化处理。

Method: 利用可信LLM对认证的非结构化数据进行语义推理生成凭证；形式化定义SCAE问题（操纵认证数据获取误导凭证的鲁棒性）和ACPP问题（通过对抗性模型选择导致隐私泄露）；构建支持金融、健康、邮件和代码等数据源的原型系统。

Result: 原型系统支持从实时金融、健康、邮件和代码源颁发凭证；基于真实金融数据的产品专业凭证上实证研究了SCAE和ACPP威胁。

Conclusion: πCreds利用LLM扩展了可验证凭证的适用范围（包括对专有软件属性的证明），同时系统性地形式化了LLM引入的新安全威胁，为去中心化可验证凭证系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Decentralized verifiable credential systems have seen limited deployment in practice. Existing constructions, built on zero-knowledge proofs, are complex, application-specific, and largely restricted to predicates over structured data.
  We present Privately Inferred Credentials ($π$Creds): privacy-preserving, legacy-compatible, decentralized verifiable credentials generated by trusted LLM inference over authenticated data. LLMs' ability to semantically reason over unstructured data substantially expands the range of claims $π$Creds can certify over existing credential systems.
  The use of LLMs also introduces new application-level threats, which we formalize through two problems: the Source-Constrained Adversarial Example (SCAE) problem, which captures robustness against adversaries that manipulate authenticated data to obtain misleading credentials, and the Authenticated Covert Predicate Poisoning (ACPP) problem, which captures privacy leakage through adversarial model selection.
  We characterize applications of $π$Creds over user data, and a novel class of credentials over proprietary software that certifies properties of a service without revealing its source code. Our prototype supports issuing credentials over live financial, health, email, and code sources, and we empirically study the SCAE and ACPP threats on a product expertise credential over real financial data.

</details>


### [136] [Collision Resistance of Single-Layer Neural Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03807)
*Marco Benedetti,Andrej Bogdanov,Enrico M. Malatesta,Marc Mézard,Gianmarco Perrupato,Alon Rosen,Nikolaj I. Schwartzbach,Riccardo Zecchina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层二元神经网络中寻找碰撞的算法复杂度，识别了阈值尺度 κ=Θ(1/√α) 作为两个互补现象的分界点，并首次使用重叠间隙属性（OGP）作为抗碰撞性的严格判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单层二元神经网络中寻找碰撞的算法复杂度，特别是理解不同阈值尺度下碰撞空间的结构差异。

Method: 通过识别阈值尺度 κ=Θ(1/√α) 作为分界点，在 κ≪1/√α 时给出简单在线算法，在 κ≫1/√α 时利用重叠间隙属性（OGP）证明指数级下界。

Result: 当 κ≪1/√α 时，存在高效在线算法可产生大量碰撞；当 κ≫1/√α 时，针对自然随机非周期激活函数，碰撞空间表现出OGP，在线算法存在指数级下界。

Conclusion: 首次将OGP作为抗碰撞性的严格判据，揭示了碰撞寻找与平均情况搜索的关键区别在于碰撞寻找具有"最坏情况"特性；将下界推广到更广泛的算法类别仍是开放方向。

Abstract: We initiate the study of the algorithmic complexity of finding collisions in single-layer binary neural networks. Given a random matrix $\mathbf{A} \in \mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$, an input $\mathbf{x} \in \{-1,1\}^n$ is mapped to a binary output vector $\varphi(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{x})\in \{-1,1\}^m$, where $\varphi$ is an activation function with constant behavior on $[κ, \infty)$ for some threshold $κ\geq 0$.
  We identify the threshold scale $κ=Θ(1/\sqrtα)$, where $α=m/n$, as separating two complementary phenomena. When $κ\ll 1/\sqrtα$, we give a simple online algorithm that efficiently produces extensive collisions. When $κ\gg 1/\sqrtα$, for a natural \emph{randomized} non-periodic activation and suitable oscillation complexity, we prove that the extensive-collision space exhibits an overlap gap property (OGP), yielding an exponential lower bound against online algorithms.
  Ours is the first work to use the overlap gap property as a rigorous criterion for collision resistance. The key difference between collision finding and average-case search is that collision finding has a new ``worst-case'' aspect: the collision finder has full control over the choice of colliding pairs. Our lower bound is proved in the online model; extending such guarantees to broader classes of algorithms, including spectral, algebraic, lattice-based, or quantum methods, remains an open direction.

</details>


### [137] [AI Agents Enable Adaptive Computer Worms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03811)
*Jonas Guan,Tom Blanchard,Hanna Foerster,Hengrui Jia,Gabriel Huang,Nicolas Papernot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由AI代理驱动的新型计算机蠕虫，它利用开源大语言模型在受感染机器上进行自主推理和攻击策略生成，能够针对不同目标定制攻击方式，且攻击者边际成本为零，对现有网络安全防御体系构成根本性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蠕虫（如WannaCry）依赖预设漏洞，可通过打补丁阻断传播。然而，AI代理的出现使蠕虫能够为每个目标生成定制化攻击策略，形成全新的安全威胁，需要提前预警和应对。

Method: 蠕虫寄生性地利用被攻陷机器运行开源大语言模型（LLMs）来维持推理能力并扩展攻击范围；在包含Linux、Windows和IoT设备的网络环境中，利用真实的企业网络常见漏洞进行传播；完全依赖窃取的计算资源而非商业AI平台。

Result: 该AI驱动的蠕虫能够在异构网络环境中成功传播，且攻击者的新增感染边际成本为零；由于不使用商业AI平台，集中式安全控制（如服务拒绝或速率限制）对其无效。

Conclusion: 自主生成式对抗实体（即能够自主推理、适应观察并实时合成攻击逻辑的恶意软件系统）已不再是理论概念，网络安全界必须为此做好准备。

Abstract: A computer worm is malware that spreads on a network by replicating itself from one machine to another. Traditional worms, like WannaCry, exploited predetermined vulnerabilities, and their spread can be halted by patching those vulnerabilities. Here we show that artificial intelligence (AI) agents enable a fundamentally new threat: a worm that generates tailored attack strategies to each target it encounters. The worm parasitically uses compromised machines to run open-weight large language models (LLMs) to sustain its reasoning, or extend its reach for further attacks. Deployed on a network of machines spanning Linux, Windows, and IoT (Internet of Things) devices, the worm propagated by exploiting common, real-world corporate network vulnerabilities. Since the worm is powered by stolen compute, the attacker's marginal cost per new infection is zero. This creates a destabilizing economic asymmetry between attackers and defenders. Moreover, because the worm requires no commercial AI platform, centralized safety controls, such as service refusals or rate limiting, are structurally irrelevant. Our results demonstrate that self-sustaining AI-driven cyber-threats are no longer theoretical. We must prepare for autonomous generative adversaries: malware systems that propagate without human operators and are defined not by fixed exploit code, but by the capacity to reason about targets, adapt to observations, and synthesize attack logic in real time.

</details>


### [138] [FlowGuard: Flow Matching for Identity-Independent Detection of Data-Free Model Stealing Attacks on Energy System Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03430)
*Maxime Schwarzer,Laurin Holz,Tobias Huerten,Johannes Loevenich,Thies Moehlenhof,Roberto Rigolin F. Lopes,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对能源基础设施中AI入侵检测系统的模型窃取攻击，本文提出了FlowGuard——一种基于流匹配的、不依赖身份信息的防御方法。该方法利用连续归一化流对合法流量进行建模，通过检测查询流量的对数似然值是否偏低（即属于分布外样本）来识别合成查询，从而防御数据无关的模型窃取攻击。实验表明，在单客户端和100客户端Sybil攻击场景下，FlowGuard相比PRADA（检测率降至0%）保持稳定检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御模型窃取攻击的方法存在两大缺陷：基于身份的查询监控无法应对分布式Sybil攻击；基于软标签扰动的预测投毒不适用于硬标签IDS部署。因此亟需一种不依赖身份信息的通用防御方案。

Method: 提出FlowGuard，核心思路是利用连续归一化流（Continuous Normalizing Flow）对合法网络流量进行密度估计。由于数据无关的模型窃取攻击生成的合成查询位于低维流形，其对数似然值显著低于真实流量，因此可通过阈值判定将分布外（OOD）查询在进入IDS处理前拦截。

Result: 在单客户端和100客户端Sybil分布式设置下，针对PRADA和FDINet使用MAZE和DisGUIDE攻击进行评估。当数据分布变化时，PRADA的检测率降至0%，而FlowGuard在两种设置下均保持稳定的检测率。

Conclusion: FlowGuard作为不依赖身份信息的防御方法，能有效抵御分布式模型窃取攻击，在分布变化时保持鲁棒性。讨论了方法的适用范围、局限性及对数据依赖型攻击的潜在应用。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) deployed in energy infrastructure are vulnerable to model theft attacks, which allow adversaries to create evasive traffic offline. Current defences against model extraction rely either on identity-bound query monitoring, which is ineffective against distributed attackers (Sybil), or on prediction poisoning through soft-label perturbation, which is inapplicable to hard-label IDS deployments. Therefore, we propose FlowGuard, an identity-independent defence based on flow matching that classifies incoming queries as out-of-distribution (OOD) prior to IDS processing. This approach exploits the fact that queries generated synthetically for data-free model stealing attacks occupy a lower-dimensional manifold than real network traffic. This results in measurably lower log-likelihoods when using a Continuous Normalizing Flow that has been trained on legitimate data. We evaluate our method against PRADA and FDINet using MAZE and DisGUIDE attacks in single-client and distributed (100-client Sybil) settings. While PRADA's detection rate dropped to 0% when the distribution changed, our defence maintained a stable detection rate across both settings without relying on identity information. We discuss the scope and limitations of the approach, and outline potential applications to data-dependent attacks.

</details>


### [139] [Learn from Your Mistakes: Tree-like Self-Play for Secure Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03489)
*Wenqi Chen,Ziyan Zhang,Bing Wang,Lin Liu,Hengheng Zhang,Zhengsu Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Tree-like Self-Play (TSP)框架，将安全代码生成重构为细粒度序列决策过程，通过构建决策树让模型在关键节点自我博弈和纠错，显著提升代码安全性并实现跨语言泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在代码生成中易复现训练数据中的安全漏洞，而SFT/RL等对齐技术仅在序列级进行粗粒度优化，无法处理局部化、单token级别的安全缺陷。

Method: TSP将代码生成建模为细粒度序列决策过程，构建决策树探索安全"黄金路径"和有漏洞变体；通过自我博弈让模型在关键决策节点上严格区分自身产生的局部错误，获得密集的on-policy学习信号。

Result: CodeLlama-7B在Python安全基准上SPR@1达75.8%（SFT仅57.0%）；未见过的CWE类别漏洞减少24.5%；成功将从C/C++学到的安全知识迁移到Python/Go/JavaScript。

Conclusion: TSP不仅记忆安全补丁，而是内化了抽象的、语言无关的安全逻辑，实现了鲁棒的分布外泛化。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in code generation, they remain prone to replicating subtle yet critical vulnerabilities endemic to their training data. Current alignment techniques, such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL), typically apply coarse-grained optimization at the sequence level. This approach often fails to address the localized nature of security flaws, where a single incorrect token choice can compromise an entire program. To bridge this gap, we introduce Tree-like Self-Play (TSP), a framework that reframes secure code generation as a fine-grained sequential decision process. Unlike standard methods that blindly maximize likelihood, TSP constructs a decision tree where the model explores branching trajectories--generating both secure "golden paths" and vulnerable variants. By treating code generation as a self-play game, the model learns to strictly discriminate against its own localized errors. This provides a dense, on-policy learning signal that forces self-correction precisely at the critical decision nodes where vulnerabilities typically emerge. Our experiments demonstrate that TSP fundamentally enhances model reliability. In Python security benchmarks, TSP boosts CodeLlama-7B's pass rate (SPR@1) to 75.8%, significantly outperforming SFT (57.0%) and unstructured self-play baselines. Crucially, TSP induces robust out-of-distribution generalization: the model not only reduces vulnerabilities in unseen categories (CWEs) by 24.5% but also successfully transfers security principles learned from C/C++ to diverse languages, including Python, Go, and JavaScript. This suggests that TSP does not merely memorize patches, but internalizes abstract, language-agnostic security logic.

</details>


### [140] [Black-box, Adaptive, Efficient, Transferable, Harmful, Applicable... Attacks Are All You Need to Break LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03647)
*Vincent Limbach,Jonas Dornbusch,David Lüdke,Stephan Günnemann,Leo Schwinn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出间接危害优化（IHO），一种基于掩码扩散语言模型的攻击方法，通过迭代偏好优化训练，只需黑盒访问即可高效评估LLM的越狱鲁棒性。IHO在多种防御设置下显著优于现有方法，无需针对特定防御进行调整，向标准化LLM越狱评估迈出一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分类领域已有标准化攻击（如AutoAttack）用于可靠评估对抗鲁棒性，但LLM越狱评估仍缺乏等效的标准化攻击方法。现有方法无法同时满足黑盒兼容、适用于任意防御管道且高效的要求。

Method: 采用掩码扩散语言模型作为攻击者，通过迭代偏好优化（Iterative Preference Optimization）对抗有害性判别器进行训练，仅需对目标模型进行黑盒访问。同一方法可作为强自适应攻击用于个体行为，也可作为高效摊销策略迁移至未见过的行为和目标模型，无需微调。

Result: 在包含Circuit Breaker训练模型与辅助检测器的多层防御设置下，IHO无需任何防御特定适配，即显著提升了攻击成功率，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: IHO被定位为向标准化LLM越狱评估迈出的实际一步，有潜力像AutoAttack对图像分类那样提升LLM安全评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Accurately evaluating adversarial robustness is a longstanding challenge. A flawed attack design can inflate robustness estimates, making deployment risk assessment and defense comparison unreliable. Historically, standardized attacks such as AutoAttack have largely resolved this for image classifiers, providing a reliable evaluation baseline for systematic comparison across defenses. However, no equivalent exists for LLM jailbreak evaluation yet, where designing such an attack is considerably more difficult. A reliable attack must, among other things, be black-box compatible, applicable to arbitrary defense pipelines, and efficient, which no existing method jointly satisfies. We introduce Indirect Harm Optimization (IHO), a masked diffusion language model attacker trained via iterative preference optimization against a harmfulness judge, requiring only black-box access to the target. The same method can be used without modification as a strong adaptive attack on individual behaviors, or as an efficient amortized policy that transfers to held-out behaviors and unseen target models without fine-tuning. Even against layered defenses, such as a Circuit Breaker-trained model combined with an auxiliary detector, IHO improves attack success considerably over state-of-the-art approaches, without any defense-specific adaptation. Our results position IHO as a practical step toward the kind of standardized jailbreak evaluation that has improved reliability in the past. Code and models are available on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Visual Graph Scaffolds for Structural Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02673)
*Runlin Lei,Xiaokui Xiao,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究图结构不仅作为LLM的外部知识源，更可作为内部推理组织工具。实验发现，以可视化图（思维导图）形式引导模型推理比将图结构平面化为文本更有效，尤其在去除直接答案提示后，视觉图引导仍能保持推理效率和答案质量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索图结构是否不仅能为LLM提供外部知识，还能作为内部推理组织形式（类似人类思维导图），以提升模型在多跳问答任务上的结构化推理能力。

Method: 在多跳问答任务中，将教师模型提供的推理轨迹重写为图结构思维导图，分别以文本扁平化形式和可视化图形式引导学生模型，对比两种引导方式在有无直接答案线索时的表现，并通过监督微调和KL散度蒸馏进行验证。

Result: 图结构扁平化为文本后，一旦去除直接答案提示，其收益大幅下降，推理效率和答案质量均显著降低；而可视化图引导在无直接答案线索时仍保持有效，该优势在监督微调和KL蒸馏后依然存在。

Conclusion: 图不仅应作为LLM的外部知识结构来研究，更应作为组织推理的可视化支架来利用，可视化形式对推理组织具有不可替代的优势。

Abstract: Graphs have been used to enhance large language models (LLMs) for structured reasoning, mostly as external knowledge sources are provided to models at test time. In this paper, we take a different view: the value of graphs for LLMs lie not only in supplying information, but also in organizing reasoning. Inspired by how humans use graph-structured mind maps to organize branching and converging thoughts, we ask whether graphs can serve as an internal form of reasoning assistance. We study this question on multi-hop question answering tasks, where teacher-provided reasoning traces are rewritten as graph mind maps and used to guide a student model. Our experiments reveal a clear modality gap. When graph structures are flattened into text, their benefits become limited once direct answer hints are removed. Under this abstract guidance setting, both reasoning efficiency and answer quality degrade substantially. In contrast, visual graph guidance remains effective without direct answer clues, and its advantage persists after supervised fine-tuning and KL-based distillation. The above findings support the claim that graphs should be studied not only as external knowledge structures for LLMs, but also as visual scaffolds for organizing reasoning.

</details>


### [142] [AURA: Action-Gated Memory for Robot Policies at Constant VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02775)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AURA-Mem是一种针对机器人具身智能体设计的恒定大小循环记忆机制，通过动作驱动的门控策略决定何时写入记忆，在保持与KV-cache相当性能的同时，将写入次数减少5-7倍，推理状态固定为4,224字节，解决了KV-cache在边缘设备上内存增长和写入耐久性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心推理场景中KV-cache通过批处理和重置大量短请求来摊销注意力缓存，但具身智能体在带宽受限的边缘硬件上运行单个长周期、非重置的交互序列，高带宽内存和闪存稀缺，闪存写入耐久性有限，内存写入而非计算成为主要约束。

Method: AURA-Mem封装了一个冻结的视觉-语言-动作骨干网络，附加恒定大小的循环记忆和一个学习得到的门控机制，仅当当前观测会改变下一个动作时才写入记忆。该门控不基于重建误差，而是直接通过闭环动作误差信号进行训练。推理状态固定为4,224字节。

Result: 在合成基准测试中，AURA-Mem匹配最佳O(1)基线的精度，同时减少5.19-6.13倍写入次数；在更简单配置下减少高达9.19倍。预算匹配的随机和周期性调度无法恢复该增益。在LIBERO-Long上的OpenVLA-OFT 7B模型上，AURA-Mem匹配无门控基础策略（0.233）并略优于始终写入的KV分支（0.217），同时减少7.0倍写入次数并保持恒定内存。KV-cache在100,000步时比AURA-Mem大6,061倍。

Conclusion: AURA-Mem在机器人具身智能体的长周期运行场景中，通过动作驱动的记忆门控机制实现了近乎无损的性能与大幅度的写入效率提升，验证了在边缘硬件上恒定大小记忆取代增长型KV-cache的可行性。论文还展示了近似信息状态价值损失界限的方法论示范，但在此规模下界限是空洞的而非保证。

Abstract: The KV-cache is the right memory for datacenters but the wrong memory for robots. Datacenter inference batches many short requests and resets them, amortizing an attention cache across a crowd. Embodied agents instead run one long, non-resetting episode on bandwidth-limited edge hardware, where high-bandwidth memory and flash are scarce, flash has finite write endurance, and memory writes rather than compute can become the binding constraint.
  AURA-Mem (Action-Utility Recurrent Adaptive Memory) targets this regime. It wraps a frozen vision-language-action backbone with a constant-size recurrent memory and a learned gate that writes only when the current observation would change the next action: memory that knows when to stay silent. Unlike reconstruction-based memory, the gate is trained directly against a closed-loop action-error signal. Its inference state is fixed at 4,224 bytes regardless of horizon, while a KV-cache grows to 6,061 times larger at 100,000 steps.
  On a controlled synthetic benchmark, AURA-Mem matches the best O(1) baseline in accuracy while using 5.19-6.13 times fewer writes, and up to 9.19 times fewer writes on easier configurations. Budget-matched random and periodic schedules do not recover this gain, isolating the benefit to the action-surprise signal. On a trained closed-loop OpenVLA-OFT 7B panel on LIBERO-Long (n=60 episodes per arm), the gate does not hurt success: AURA-Mem matches the ungated base policy (0.233) and slightly exceeds an always-write KV arm (0.217), while using 7.0 times fewer writes and constant memory. We also instantiate an approximate-information-state value-loss bound as a methodology demonstration; at this scale, the bound is vacuous rather than a guarantee.

</details>


### [143] [Evaluating Transformer and LSTM Frameworks for Prediction in Ungauged Basins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02791)
*Taye Akinrele,James Halgren,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了仅编码器Transformer与LSTM在上游径流推断任务中的表现，发现LSTM整体性能更优。结合下游水文信息可使模型性能中位数NNSE提升超过60%。研究认为循环记忆架构更适合该任务，而下游水文上下文提供了强大的辅助约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未测流域中，缺乏直接观测数据增加了水文预测的不确定性，限制了极端事件的预估能力。本研究旨在评估Transformer架构是否比LSTM更适合在有限水文信息条件下进行上游径流推断。

Method: 使用NOAA国家水模型（NWM）的回顾性模拟数据，比较仅编码器Transformer与LSTM在两种配置（仅上游信息、上下游信息结合）下的上游径流推断性能。

Result: LSTM在所有配置下整体表现优于Transformer；引入下游信息后所有模型性能大幅提升，中位数NNSE增加超过60%。

Conclusion: 循环记忆（LSTM）比仅编码器Transformer更适合上游径流重建任务；下游水文上下文提供了强辅助约束，能显著提升不同架构的预测能力。

Abstract: Watershed networks exhibit convergent topologies in which multiple tributaries merge into downstream channels,integrating diverse upstream hydrological processes. In ungauged basins, the absence of direct observations increases uncertainty and limits the ability to anticipate extreme events. This study evaluates whether an encoder-only Transformer provides an advantage over an LSTM for upstream streamflow inference under limited hydrologic information, using retrospective simulations from the NOAA National Water Model (NWM). Across both upstream-only and combined configurations, the LSTM showed stronger overall performance than the Transformer model across the two configurations. Incorporating downstream information further boosted performance for all models, increasing median NNSE by more than 60%. Rather than treating this as a leaderboard-style comparison, we interpret the experiments as a test of architectural inductive bias for hydrologic sequence inference. The results indicate that recurrent memory remains better aligned with this upstream reconstruction task than an encoder-only Transformer, while downstream hydrologic context provides a strong auxiliary constraint that substantially improves prediction skill across architectures

</details>


### [144] [BehaviorBench: Modeling Real-World User Decisions from Behavioral Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02798)
*Liangwei Yang,Jielin Qiu,Zixiang Chen,Ming Zhu,Juntao Tan,Zhiwei Liu,Wenting Zhao,Zhujun Lan,Akshara Prabhakar,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BehaviorBench是一个基于真实世界链上和预测市场行为数据构建的个性化决策建模基准，包含141,445个信念预测实例和1,485,972个交易预测实例，用于评估生成模型在四种历史接口下的个性化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户理解基准多依赖模拟用户或模型生成行为，但研究表明模型模拟可能与真实人类行为存在系统性偏差，因此需要基于真实行为轨迹的评估基准。

Method: 从预测市场和链上记录重构钱包级决策历史，构建信念预测（预测用户最终立场和置信度）和交易预测（预测交易方向和数量）两个互补任务层，包含2,000个评估钱包，采用四种历史接口（无个性化、直接近期历史、生成用户画像、检索支持钱包证据）评估前沿和开源生成模型。

Result: 个性化对信念预测的改善比交易预测更一致；模型排名在不同任务层和指标上存在差异；不同历史接口暴露出不同的失败模式。

Conclusion: BehaviorBench提供了一个基于真实世界行为证据而非仅依赖模拟用户来研究个性化方法的评估环境。

Abstract: Many decision-support settings require systems that adapt to individual users, but evaluation data for this problem remain limited. Existing benchmarks for user understanding often rely on simulated users or model-generated behavior, even though recent work cautions that model-based simulations can diverge systematically from human behavior. We introduce \textsc{BehaviorBench}, a benchmark for evaluating personalized decision modeling from real-world behavioral traces. \textsc{BehaviorBench} reconstructs wallet-level decision histories from observed public prediction-market and on-chain records, and organizes them into two complementary task layers: \emph{Belief prediction}, which predicts a user's final revealed stance and confidence in a market, and \emph{Trade prediction}, which predicts the direction and amount of individual transactions. Across 2,000 evaluation wallets, the benchmark contains 141,445 Belief instances and 1,485,972 Trade instances, with disjoint support pools for retrieval-based evaluation. We evaluate frontier and open-weight generative models under four history interfaces: no personalization, direct recent history, generated user profiles, and retrieved support-wallet evidence. Personalization improves Belief prediction more consistently than Trade prediction, model rankings change across task layers and metrics, and different history interfaces expose different failure modes. \textsc{BehaviorBench} provides an evaluation setting for studying whether personalized methods can use real-world behavioral evidence rather than simulated users alone.

</details>


### [145] [ChatHealthAI: Aligning Electronic Health Record Representations with Large Language Models for Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02802)
*Bo-Hong Wang,Baicheng Peng,Ruilin Wang,Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChatHealthAI是一个多模态推理框架，通过任务感知重采样器将预训练的EHR基础模型与冻结的大语言模型对齐，实现在结构化纵向电子健康记录上的可解释临床推理，在EHRSHOT基准测试中展示了竞争性的预测性能和更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在临床决策支持中具有自然语言推理能力，但难以有效建模结构化的纵向电子健康记录；而EHR基础模型可以学习预测性患者表征，但缺乏可解释的基于语言的推理。为了弥合这一差距，需要一种能够结合两者优势的框架。

Method: 提出ChatHealthAI框架，通过任务感知重采样器（task-aware resampler）将预训练的EHR基础模型生成的结构化患者表征与冻结的大语言模型的语义空间对齐，同时整合纵向患者表征与精细化的临床事件描述，实现临床可解释的自然语言推理。

Result: 在EHRSHOT基准的三个临床预测任务上，ChatHealthAI在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，改善了推理质量和可解释性。

Conclusion: 将EHR基础模型与预训练的大语言模型相结合，有望实现可解释的临床预测，为临床决策支持提供更可靠的AI辅助工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong natural-language reasoning abilities for clinical decision support, but struggle to effectively model structured longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs). In contrast, EHR foundation models can learn predictive patient representations, yet lack interpretable language-based reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ChatHealthAI, a multimodal reasoning framework that aligns structured EHR representations from a pretrained EHR foundation model with the semantic space of a frozen LLM through a task-aware resampler. By integrating longitudinal patient representations with refined clinical event descriptions, ChatHealthAI enables clinically grounded natural-language reasoning while maintaining accurate patient prediction. We evaluated ChatHealthAI on three clinical predictive tasks from the EHRSHOT benchmark. Results show that ChatHealthAI improves reasoning quality and interpretability while preserving competitive predictive performance. These findings highlight the potential of integrating EHR foundation models with pretrained LLMs for interpretable clinical prediction.

</details>


### [146] [Traj-Evolve: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Patient Trajectory Modeling in Lung Cancer Early Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02812)
*Sihang Zeng,Matthew Thompson,Ruth Etzioni,Meliha Yetisgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Traj-Evolve是一个自进化的多智能体系统，通过经验池（ExPool）和多智能体强化学习（MARL）两种互补机制，从纵向电子健康记录中建模患者轨迹。在肺癌预测任务上，它优于9个强基线，并揭示了两种机制的互补性：ExPool提高特异性，MARL提高敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的多智能体系统虽然解决了长上下文问题，但孤立地处理患者，无法像临床医生那样利用来自相似既往病例的累积经验，缺乏跨患者的经验迁移能力。

Method: 提出了两种互补的进化机制：(1) 经验池（ExPool）：作为非参数化记忆，通过拒绝采样索引推理轨迹，检索相似患者作为少样本上下文；(2) 多智能体强化学习（MARL）：通过奖励排序的微调参数化地优化智能体间和智能体-记忆协作。采用留一法交叉检索策略统一两者。

Result: 在利用长达五年多模态EHR数据的肺癌预测任务上，Traj-Evolve在整体人群和具有挑战性的从不吸烟人群中均优于9个强基线。分析发现：ExPool扩展使最优检索从多样样本转向特定样本；MARL下管理智能体损失快速收敛而工作智能体持续受益于更多验证患者；ExPool提升特异性而MARL提升敏感性。

Conclusion: Traj-Evolve通过结合非参数化记忆和参数化优化的自进化机制，有效解决了EHR轨迹建模中的稀疏、噪声和长上下文问题。两种机制在风险预测上互补，为临床决策支持提供了更鲁棒的方法。

Abstract: Modeling patient trajectories from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) requires reasoning over sparse, noisy, and long-context multimodal sequences. Existing LLM-based multi-agent systems address context length but process patients in isolation, failing to mirror how clinicians leverage accumulated experience from similar prior cases. We present Traj-Evolve, a self-evolving multi-agent system with two complementary evolving mechanisms. First, an Experience Pool (ExPool) acts as a non-parametric memory, indexing rejection-sampled reasoning traces to retrieve similar patients as few-shot contexts. Second, multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) via reward-ranked fine-tuning parametrically optimizes inter-agent and agent-memory collaboration. A leave-one-out cross-retrieval strategy unifies the two, aligning training- and inference-time behavior under retrieval augmentation. On a lung cancer prediction task utilizing up to five years of multimodal EHRs, Traj-Evolve outperforms 9 strong baselines on the overall population and a challenging never-smoker population. Analysis of the evolving dynamics highlights three key findings: (1) expanding the ExPool shifts optimal retrieval from diverse to specific samples; (2) under MARL, the manager agent's prediction loss converges quickly while the worker agents' temporal reasoning continues to benefit from more verified patients; and (3) the two mechanisms are complementary on the predicted risk, where ExPool improves specificity while MARL improves sensitivity.

</details>


### [147] [An Exploration of Collision-based Enemy Morphology Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02832)
*Johor Jara Gonzalez,Matthew Guzdial*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了三种基于玩家碰撞信息生成敌人形态的新方法，所有方法在性能上均优于从机器人形态学工作改编的进化基线，但各有优缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管程序化内容生成（PCG）已有大量研究，但针对游戏中敌人生成的工作很少，尤其是敌人形态（基本身体结构或碰撞信息）的生成几乎空白，而机器人领域已有相关形态生成研究。

Method: 提出了三种不同的新方法，基于玩家碰撞信息来生成敌人形态，并与来自机器人形态学工作的进化基线方法进行性能对比。

Result: 三种方法均表现出与进化基线相当或更优的性能，但各自具有不同的优点和缺点。

Conclusion: 本文填补了游戏中敌人形态生成研究的空白，验证了从玩家碰撞信息出发生成敌人形态的可行性，并为未来研究提供了多种有潜力的方向。
